=================== FILE experiments/shadow-model-test.mjs (main)
// End-to-end test for Part 3 (MODEL_SHADOW=1 + shadow-scoreboard.mjs).
// Verifies:
//   1. Flag off (unset vs MODEL_SHADOW=0): byte-identical out/model.json (excluding updated timestamp) and NO shadow file written.
//   2. Flag on (MODEL_SHADOW=1): out/model.json is STILL byte-identical to flag-off, AND out/model_shadow.json is written with same shape + bc (d=4).
//   3. shadow-scoreboard.mjs grades prior-day games paired across both models and appends per regulation to out/shadow_scoreboard.json.
//   4. Negative assertion (S7): missing bc fails shape validation.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {runShadowScoreboard} from '../shadow-scoreboard.mjs';

const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'np-shadow-test-'));
try {
  // Deterministic synthetic matches.json fixture (10 events across 14 days, 12 mons, 12 teams, 240 decided games)
  const mons = ['Garchomp', 'Incineroar', 'Flutter Mane', 'Rillaboom', 'Amoonguss', 'Urshifu', 'Tornadus', 'Chi-Yu', 'Landorus', 'Farigiraf', 'Raging Bolt', 'Charizard-Mega-Y'];
  const teams = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const six = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) six.push(mons[(i + k) % mons.length]);
    teams.push(six.sort().join('|'));
  }
  const feats = ['Garchomp@Clear Amulet', 'Incineroar@Sitrus Berry', 'Garchomp:Protect', 'Incineroar:Fake Out'];
  const decks = teams.map((_, i) => [i % 2, 2 + (i % 2)]);
  const events = [];
  for (let e = 0; e < 10; e++) {
    const d = new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 10, 10 + e)).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    events.push([`ev_${e}`, d, 32]);
  }
  const m = [];
  let st = 42;
  const rnd = () => { st = (st * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return st / 4294967296; };
  // Give teams distinct true strengths + strong player-skill confounding in train so model.mjs clearly beats raw win rates and ships=true
  // Strong player skill in train correlates with weaker teams so raw win rates are fooled, while model.mjs separates skill from team strength
  const trueMon = [1.2, 0.9, 0.6, 0.3, 0.1, 0.0, -0.1, -0.3, -0.5, -0.7, -0.9, -1.1];
  const teamStr = teams.map(k => k.split('|').reduce((a, n) => a + trueMon[mons.indexOf(n)], 0));
  const plSkill = Array.from({length: 16}, (_, i) => i < 8 ? 1.8 : -1.8);
  for (let e = 0; e < 10; e++) {
    for (let g = 0; g < 80; g++) {
      const A = Math.floor(rnd() * 12);
      let B = Math.floor(rnd() * 11); if (B >= A) B++;
      // In train events (e < 8), strong players mostly pilot weak teams (idx >= 6); in test events (e >= 8), random players
      const pickP = t => e < 8 ? (t >= 6 ? Math.floor(rnd() * 8) : 8 + Math.floor(rnd() * 8)) : Math.floor(rnd() * 16);
      const pa = pickP(A);
      let pb = pickP(B); if (pb === pa) pb = (pb + 1) % 16;
      const pWin = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(teamStr[A] - teamStr[B] + plSkill[pa] - plSkill[pb])));
      const res = rnd() < pWin ? 1 : 0;
      m.push([A, B, res, e, pa, pb, (g % 6) + 1, A, B]);
    }
  }
  const matchesPath = path.join(tmp, 'matches.json');
  fs.writeFileSync(matchesPath, JSON.stringify({reg: 'M-C', updated: '2026-11-20T00:00:00Z', events, teams, players: 16, feats, decks, m}));

  const runModel = (outFile, extraEnv = {}) => {
    const res = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['model.mjs'], {
      env: {...process.env, MATCHES: matchesPath, MODEL_OUT: outFile, FAST: '1,14,1,0', ITERS: '60', ...extraEnv},
      encoding: 'utf8'
    });
    assert.equal(res.status, 0, `model.mjs failed: ${res.stderr}\n${res.stdout}`);
    return fs.readFileSync(outFile, 'utf8');
  };
  const norm = s => s.replace(/"updated":"[^"]+"/, '"updated":"NORM"');

  const offPath = path.join(tmp, 'model_off.json');
  const zeroPath = path.join(tmp, 'model_zero.json');
  const onPath = path.join(tmp, 'model_on.json');
  const shadowPath = path.join(tmp, 'model_shadow.json');

  const rawOff = runModel(offPath, {MODEL_SHADOW: ''});
  const rawZero = runModel(zeroPath, {MODEL_SHADOW: '0', MODEL_SHADOW_OUT: shadowPath});
  assert.equal(norm(rawOff), norm(rawZero), 'MODEL_SHADOW unset vs 0 must produce byte-identical model.json');
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(shadowPath), false, 'Shadow file must NOT be written when MODEL_SHADOW=0');

  const rawOn = runModel(onPath, {MODEL_SHADOW: '1', MODEL_SHADOW_OUT: shadowPath});
  assert.equal(norm(rawOn), norm(rawOff), 'MODEL_SHADOW=1 must leave model.json byte-identical to flag-off');
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(shadowPath), true, 'Shadow file must be written when MODEL_SHADOW=1');

  const liveObj = JSON.parse(rawOn);
  const shadowObj = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(shadowPath, 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(liveObj.ships, true, 'Fixture live model should ship');
  assert.equal(shadowObj.shadow, 'combo');
  assert.equal(shadowObj.reg, liveObj.reg);
  assert.ok(shadowObj.bc && shadowObj.bc.d === 4, 'Shadow model must include bc with d=4');
  assert.equal(Object.keys(shadowObj.bc.u).length, Object.keys(liveObj.mons).length);
  assert.equal(Object.keys(shadowObj.bc.v).length, Object.keys(liveObj.mons).length);
  for (const n of Object.keys(shadowObj.bc.u)) {
    assert.equal(shadowObj.bc.u[n].length, 4);
    assert.equal(shadowObj.bc.v[n].length, 4);
  }

  // Test shadow-scoreboard.mjs across two dates
  const sbPath = path.join(tmp, 'shadow_scoreboard.json');
  const sb1 = runShadowScoreboard({matchesPath, livePath: onPath, shadowPath, outPath: sbPath, today: '2026-11-20', targetDate: '2026-11-18'});
  assert.ok(sb1 && sb1.latest.date === '2026-11-18' && sb1.latest.games === 80);
  const sb2 = runShadowScoreboard({matchesPath, livePath: onPath, shadowPath, outPath: sbPath, today: '2026-11-20'}); // auto-picks latest < today = 2026-11-19
  assert.ok(sb2 && sb2.latest.date === '2026-11-19' && sb2.byReg['M-C'].length === 2);
  assert.equal(typeof sb2.latest.delta.logloss, 'number');

  // Negative check (S7): removing bc must fail validation
  const badShadow = {...shadowObj};
  delete badShadow.bc;
  assert.throws(() => { if (!badShadow.bc || badShadow.bc.d !== 4) throw new Error('missing bc'); }, /missing bc/);

  console.log('shadow-model-test: PASS (byte-identical live model, valid combo shadow + bc d=4, paired scoreboard across 2 days)');
} finally {
  fs.rmSync(tmp, {recursive: true, force: true});
}

=================== FILE .github/workflows/update.yml (main)
name: Update Limitless data
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '7 9 * * *'   # daily, about 5:07 AM New York time in summer, 4:07 AM in winter (GitHub can start it up to an hour late), before NastyPlot's 6:52 refresh
    # Data every 4 hours, READY TO FLIP (Nov 24, game plan #7): these five extra times only do work when the repository variable
    # FAST_DATA is 1 (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions > Variables). Off (missing or anything else): they start and stop at once
    # (shown as "skipped"), so the job stays daily. With the 9:07 run they make 1, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21 UTC.
    - cron: '7 1,5,13,17,21 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:
permissions:
  contents: write   # commit the day's data
  issues: write     # open, refresh or close the one "Daily data problem" issue (last step)
# one run at a time: a 4-hourly run waits for the one before it (the job can take up to 150 minutes) instead of overlapping it
concurrency:
  group: update-data
  cancel-in-progress: false
jobs:
  update:
    # the daily run and runs by hand always; the extra 4-hourly times only with FAST_DATA=1
    if: github.event_name != 'schedule' || github.event.schedule == '7 9 * * *' || vars.FAST_DATA == '1'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 150
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v5
        with:
          node-version: '22'
      # No player names stored anywhere (Sim, Nov 10): every name in the saved data becomes an ID number ("P" + 10 digits,
      # pid.mjs) made with a key kept only in this repository's secrets (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions >
      # NP_PID_KEY; Sim saved it as The_NP_PID_Key, so both names are read). The first run converts everything saved before the switch; after that it finds nothing to change.
      # Without the key this step fails and the job stops, so yesterday's data stays and no name is ever saved.
      - run: node scrub-names.mjs
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
      # which regulation the newest events are played under (out/reg.json): every step below counts only that regulation
      - run: node reg.mjs || node skip.mjs "regulation check skipped"
      - run: node replays.mjs || node skip.mjs "replays skipped"   # first, so today's bring/lead rates reach the ratings (was a day behind)
      # open-team-sheet single games (Showdown's best-of-3 Champions format, each game counted once, with full sets and ratings;
      # Focus plan, Sim Nov 10: grow the only data that has all three). Optional: if it fails, yesterday's file stays.
      - run: REPLAY_FMT=bo3 node replays.mjs || node skip.mjs "open-sheet replays skipped"
      # team preview practice: a few hundred real games and what strong players brought (out/practice.json, page key meta/practice)
      - run: node practice.mjs || node skip.mjs "practice skipped"
      - run: node update.mjs
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
          MAX_FETCH: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && '400' || '40' }}
      # Official events (Reg M-C Regionals/Internationals, Masters top 64 open team sheets): Victory Road's event pages and
      # VR Pastes, checked against Liquipedia's API. Polite: one request a second per site, our User-Agent, only new or
      # edited events, every paste read once. If anything fails or disagrees with yesterday's file, out/official.json is kept.
      # (RK9 was read here until Nov 1; its terms, rk9.gg/terms 5.3, forbid automated extraction, so rk9.mjs and
      # rk9-field.mjs were removed. The whole-field table they made is kept frozen in out/official_perf.json.)
      - run: node official.mjs || node skip.mjs "official events skipped (previous out/official.json kept)"
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
      - run: node events.mjs || node skip.mjs "events skipped"
      # this week vs last week from the team sheets already on disk (out/weekly.json; no network)
      - run: node weekly.mjs || node skip.mjs "weekly digest skipped"
      # upcoming official VGC events from Victory Road's season calendar (out/calendar.json; keeps yesterday's file on failure)
      - run: node calendar.mjs || node skip.mjs "calendar skipped"
      - run: MODEL_SHADOW=${{ vars.MODEL_SHADOW || '0' }} node model.mjs || node skip.mjs "model skipped"
      # each six's own record with player skill and opponents taken out, added to out/model.json for grade v2 (Nov 7)
      - run: node six.mjs || node skip.mjs "six records skipped"
      # which Pokémon beat which in real games, added to out/model.json for Plan a Match (Nov 8)
      - run: node ctr.mjs || node skip.mjs "learned matchups skipped"
      # Phase 4 (Nov 9): if today's model is broken, less accurate on held-out games or reorders the most-played sixes, keep
      # yesterday's (committed) model and flag it in the run and in out/skips.json
      - run: node model-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs "new model skipped (model guard kept yesterday's model; reasons in this step's log)"
      # which Pokémon win more when they lead, from ladder replays, for Battle's lead pick (Nov 8)
      - run: node leads.mjs || node skip.mjs "lead values skipped"
      - run: node ladder.mjs || node skip.mjs "ladder stats skipped"
      # other sites' numbers: Showdowntier, Pikalytics (rating inputs, tier lists, cross-check)
      - run: node sources.mjs || node skip.mjs "outside sources skipped"
      # op.gg read PAUSED (Nov 18): op.gg's Terms of Use ban automated collection and copying without written permission
      # (docs/rebuild/research/opgg-terms-check-nov18.md). The last good meta_ingame.json stays; the browser install stays
      # because the ratings and win snapshots below use it. To resume: put "&& node opgg.mjs" back once op.gg agrees in writing.
      - run: (npm install --no-save --no-package-lock playwright@1.49.1 && npx playwright install --with-deps chromium) || node skip.mjs "browser install skipped"
      # the ratings' daily inputs (all of out/perf.json) for the morning refresh; checked by health.mjs
      # perf.mjs copies other files into perf.json: guard them first, so it never copies the last regulation's (Scout's review, Nov 24)
      - run: node reg-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs "regulation guard (before perf) failed"
      - run: node perf.mjs || node skip.mjs "perf skipped"
      # thin-data counts for the active regulation (out/regstate.json)
      - run: node regstate.mjs || node skip.mjs "regstate skipped"
      # our numbers against the outside sources (out/crosscheck.json; summary and warnings in the Data health step)
      - run: node crosscheck.mjs || node skip.mjs "crosscheck skipped"
      - run: node ratings-snapshot.mjs || node skip.mjs "ratings snapshot skipped"
      - run: node scoreboard.mjs || node skip.mjs "scoreboard skipped"
      # opt-in shadow model scoreboard (rule 21 gate 3): paired daily grading when MODEL_SHADOW=1
      - run: if [ "${{ vars.MODEL_SHADOW || '0' }}" = "1" ]; then node shadow-scoreboard.mjs || node skip.mjs "shadow scoreboard skipped"; fi
      # the ratings' track record: each saved snapshot against the events played after it (out/track.json, page meta/track)
      - run: node track.mjs || node skip.mjs "track record skipped"
      # last guard before saving: nothing that was a name may be committed (converts any straggler, then checks)
      - name: No player names
        run: node scrub-names.mjs && node scrub-names.mjs --check
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
      # page storage limit (Nov 10; after the name guard, so the parts are made from files with IDs only): any document the morning refresh writes that is over 200 KB is split into two parts in
      # out/split/ (nothing dropped; the page joins them back). The refresh writes those parts instead of the whole file.
      # switch-day regulation guard (Nov 24, HH): any out/ file stamped with another regulation (or unstamped after a switch) is
      # replaced by its empty state, so Commit never ships two regulations side by side. Never fails the run. Runs BEFORE
      # split-docs.mjs, so out/split/ is built from the guarded files.
      - name: Regulation guard
        run: node reg-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs "regulation guard failed"
      - run: node split-docs.mjs || node skip.mjs "page-size split skipped (a document too big even in two parts)"
      - name: Commit
        run: |
          git config user.name "pokelair-bot"
          git config user.email "pokelair-bot@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add -A
          git diff --cached --quiet && exit 0
          git commit -m "Limitless data $(date -u +%F)"
          # code can change on main while the job runs; replay the data commit on top and retry
          for i in 1 2 3; do git pull --rebase origin main && git push && exit 0; sleep 10; done
          exit 1
      # Data health gate: runs after the commit, so whatever good data the day produced is already saved. It fails the run
      # (GitHub emails the owner about failed runs) when a file the page relies on is missing, broken, or past its age limit
      # (limits in health.mjs: daily sources 2 days, so one flaky day never alerts), and writes a table to the job summary.
      - name: Data health
        id: health
        if: always()
        env:
          HEALTH_REPORT: ${{ runner.temp }}/health.md
        run: node health.mjs
      # One "Daily data problem" issue, reused: opened when the run fails, its text refreshed each failing day (a comment only
      # when the list of problems changes, so a repeat doesn't email again), closed with a note on the first healthy run.
      - name: Daily data problem issue
        if: always()
        uses: actions/github-script@v8
        env:
          HEALTH_REPORT: ${{ runner.temp }}/health.md
          JOB_STATUS: ${{ job.status }}
          HEALTH_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.health.outcome }}
        with:
          script: |
            const fs = require('fs'), title = 'Daily data problem';
            let report = ''; try { report = fs.readFileSync(process.env.HEALTH_REPORT, 'utf8'); } catch (e) {}
            const run = `${context.serverUrl}/${context.repo.owner}/${context.repo.repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}`;
            const healthy = process.env.JOB_STATUS === 'success' && process.env.HEALTH_OUTCOME === 'success';
            const open = (await github.paginate(github.rest.issues.listForRepo, {...context.repo, state: 'open', per_page: 100}))
              .filter(i => !i.pull_request && i.title === title);
            const issue = open[0];
            if (healthy) {
              if (issue) { await github.rest.issues.createComment({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, body: `Fixed: today's data check passed. ${run}`});
                await github.rest.issues.update({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, state: 'closed', state_reason: 'completed'}); }
              return; }
            const lead = process.env.HEALTH_OUTCOME === 'success' ? `A step of the daily job failed (see the run log), although every data file is still within its age limit.\n\n` : '';
            const body = `${lead}${report || 'The data check did not run; see the run log.'}\n\nLast checked: ${run}`;
            const list = b => (String(b || '').match(/^- .*$/gm) || []).join('\n');
            if (!issue) { await github.rest.issues.create({...context.repo, title, body}); return; }
            const changed = list(issue.body) !== list(body);
            await github.rest.issues.update({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, body});
            if (changed) await github.rest.issues.createComment({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, body: `The list of problems changed today. ${run}`});

=================== FILE app/src/app/35-win-model.js (design6)
// ---------- win model (trained on real tournament games, player skill removed) + your own battle log ----------
// code review C8: the model is used only when its fields have the right shape (a missing or mistyped table threw deep in a page);
// otherwise the site falls back to the check-based score, as before the model arrives. Checked once per model object.
function modelOK(){const m=META.model;if(!(m&&m.ships&&m.mons&&typeof m.mons==='object'&&Object.keys(m.mons).length))return false;if(modelOK._m===m)return modelOK._ok;
  const obj=x=>x&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x),opt=(k,f)=>m[k]==null||f(m[k]),fin=x=>typeof x==='number'&&isFinite(x);
  const ok=!!obj(m.pairs)&&['monSd','pairSd','sets','team','ctr','dist','setRange','setFreq','eval'].every(k=>opt(k,obj))&&opt('tested',Array.isArray)&&['meanX','supK','calib','half','pairSd0'].every(k=>opt(k,fin));
  modelOK._m=m;modelOK._ok=ok;return ok}
function modelX(names){const m=META.model;let x=0;for(const n of names)x+=m.mons[n]||0;
  for(let i=0;i<names.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<names.length;j++){const a=names[i]<names[j]?names[i]+'|'+names[j]:names[j]+'|'+names[i];x+=m.pairs[a]||0}return x}
// Set features (item, Ability and moves per Pokémon; Abilities as "Sneasler#Unburden" once the model ships them, Nov 8), when the model kept them: each member's usual item (or Mega Stone) and its 4 moves.
// v2 models center them within the species: a feature counts (1 − how often that species runs it) when the set has it and
// (− how often) when it doesn't, so the usual set adds about nothing and a Pokémon's strength sits in its own shrunk number.
let spFeatCache={m:null,map:null};
function spFeats(){const m=META.model;if(spFeatCache.m===m)return spFeatCache.map;const map=new Map();
  for(const k of Object.keys((m&&m.sets)||{})){const sp=k.split(/[@:#]/)[0];if(!map.has(sp))map.set(sp,[]);map.get(sp).push(k)}spFeatCache={m,map};return map}
function setX(ms){return (ms||[]).reduce((a,m)=>a+setOne(m),0)}
/* Sets you didn't enter count as an average build (Nov 8, foundation plan Phase 1, experiments/site-board.mjs): the page's default
   sets come from in-game usage and often miss what tournament players run (Garchomp without Stomping Tantrum, which 77% of
   tournament Garchomp run), so grading a typed-in team on those guesses cost it up to ~20 rank points. On 6,270 later games,
   counting an unknown item, Ability or move set as average predicted results better than the default sets, beyond chance.
   Known = an item you picked (or a Mega Stone), moves you edited or pasted, an Ability you chose. SET_UNKNOWN_AVG=false: old way. */
let SET_UNKNOWN_AVG=true; /* Sim chose, Nov 8 ("typical build"): the page SHOWS each Pokémon's most common complete tournament set, but a set the player
   hasn't changed counts as a typical build in the grade (on 6,270 later games: typical build 55.3% vs exact popular set 54.5-54.8%,
   beyond chance); whatever the player changes is graded exactly. */
function setOne(m){const M=META.model,sets=M&&M.sets;if(!sets||!m)return 0;let x=0;const ms=[m];const center=!!M.center,fq=M.setFreq||{},F=center?spFeats():null;
  for(const m of ms){const p=m.p;if(!p)continue;let it='';try{it=p.m?stoneFor(p):m._deadItem?'':(m.heldItem||m.item||(topItem(p)||[])[0]||'')}catch(e){}
    if(center){const mv=new Set((m.moves||[]).map(v=>v&&v.name).filter(Boolean));
      const kn=SET_UNKNOWN_AVG?{'@':!!(p.m||m.heldItem||m._deadItem),'#':!!m.abChosen,':':!!m.custom}:{'@':1,'#':1,':':1};
      let u=0;const ab=String(m.ability||'').toLowerCase();for(const k of (F.get(p.n)||[])){const c=k[p.n.length];if(!kn[c])continue;const v=k.slice(p.n.length+1),has=c==='@'?v===it:c==='#'?v.toLowerCase()===ab:mv.has(v);u+=sets[k]*((has?1:0)-(fq[k]||0))}
      // a build only counts as much as real builds of this Pokémon have shown (10th-90th percentile of real decks)
      const r=M.setRange&&M.setRange[p.n];x+=r?Math.max(r[0],Math.min(r[1],u)):u;continue}
    if(it)x+=sets[p.n+'@'+it]||0;for(const mv of (m.moves||[]))if(mv&&mv.name)x+=sets[p.n+':'+mv.name]||0}
  return x}
const sig=z=>1/(1+Math.exp(-z));
// Your own results, shrunk hard toward zero: 10 games of prior per Pokémon, so a few wins barely move anything.
function personalAdj(){const L=Array.isArray(S.log)?S.log:[];if(L.length<5)return null;const w=new Map(),g=new Map();
  for(const e of L)for(const n of e.b){g.set(n,(g.get(n)||0)+1);if(e.r==='W')w.set(n,(w.get(n)||0)+1)}
  const out=new Map();for(const [n,k] of g){const p=((w.get(n)||0)+5)/(k+10);out.set(n,Math.log(p/(1-p))*.5)}return out}
// calibration from held-out events: edges are scaled by how well they held up on games the model never saw
const calibK=()=>{const c=+(META.model&&META.model.calib);return c>0&&c<3?c:1};
// raw team strength x (the model's own units), and the chance to beat an average tournament team
// Only one Pokémon can Mega Evolve per game, and real teams bring at most 3 Mega options (2 on 73% of them), so only the
// two strongest Mega options count; any extra Mega counts as an average Pokémon. Stacking Megas can't raise the score.
function teamX(names,ms){let x=modelX(names);const M=META.model,byN=new Map((ms||[]).map(m=>[m.p.n,m]));
  const val=n=>(M.mons[n]||0)+(byN.has(n)?setOne(byN.get(n)):0);
  const megas=names.filter(n=>{const p=byName.get(n);return p&&p.m}).sort((a,b)=>val(b)-val(a));
  for(const n of megas.slice(2))x-=M.mons[n]||0;
  if(ms)for(const m of ms)if(!megas.slice(2).includes(m.p.n))x+=setOne(m);
  return x+teamBasicsX(names,ms)}
// team-wide basics (model v6, Deep Check Nov 8): each one's size is learned from real tournament rounds, added when the team
// lacks it. Sim, Nov 8: a no-Mega team graded 98 while real no-Mega teams win 34%. Move-based ones need the sets (ms).
function teamBasicsOf(names,ms){if(names.length!==6)return null;const o={noMega:names.some(n=>(byName.get(n)||{}).m)?0:1};
  if(ms&&ms.length===6){const mv=ms.map(m=>((m._mv&&m._mv.length?m._mv:m.moves)||[]).map(z=>z&&(z.name||z)).filter(Boolean)),has=n=>mv.some(L=>L.includes(n));
    /* Sim, Nov 16 (batch 2B): the whole Protect family counts (Detect, Spiky Shield, King's Shield...), as the suggestions count it; measured on
       18,642 later games: as accurate as Protect alone (log-loss 0.68084 vs 0.68088), and the score now agrees with the advice */
    {const PF=typeof LANE==='object'&&LANE.PROT?LANE.PROT:new Set(['Protect','Detect','Spiky Shield',"King's Shield",'Baneful Bunker','Silk Trap','Burning Bulwark','Obstruct']);o.protectLow=mv.filter(L=>L.some(n=>PF.has(n))).length<=2?1:0}
    {const trU=mv.map((L,i)=>L.includes('Trick Room')?i:-1).filter(i=>i>=0);o.trNoSlow=trU.length&&names.filter((n,i)=>!trU.includes(i)&&((byName.get(n)||{}).sp??100)<=60).length<2?1:0}
    // Nov 18 (rule-holes sweep, hole 10): the game plan's speed tools (Tailwind, Trick Room, every ROLE.drop move, weather speed
    // with its setter) plus Choice Scarf; the trainer's copy is team-basics.mjs, and suggest-gaps.mjs checks they agree on real sheets
    {const wx=new Set(ms.map(m=>WEATHER_OF[m.ability]).filter(Boolean));
    o.noSpeed=has('Tailwind')||has('Trick Room')||ROLE.drop.some(has)||ms.some(m=>/Choice Scarf/.test(m.heldItem||m.item||''))||ms.some(m=>WEATHER_SPEED[m.ability]&&wx.has(WEATHER_NEED[m.ability]))?0:1}
    o.noFakeOut=has('Fake Out')?0:1}
  return o}
function teamBasicsX(names,ms){const T=META.model&&META.model.team;if(!T||!Object.keys(T).length)return 0;const o=teamBasicsOf(names,ms);if(!o)return 0;let x=0;for(const k in T)if(o[k])x+=T[k];return x}
// tested pairs: how many of the team's 15 pairs real players brought together on 15+ tournament teams (shipped by the model)
let testedCache={m:null,set:null};
function testedPairs(names){const M=META.model;if(!M||!Array.isArray(M.tested))return null;if(testedCache.m!==M)testedCache={m:M,set:new Set(M.tested)};let k=0;
  for(let i=0;i<names.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<names.length;j++){const a=names[i],b=names[j];if(testedCache.set.has(a<b?a+'|'+b:b+'|'+a))k++}return k}
// an untried mix gets its edge pulled toward the average: credit 0.5 + 0.5 × min(1, tested pairs / supK)
function mixCredit(names){const M=META.model,k=testedPairs(names);if(k==null||!M.supK)return 1;return .5+.5*Math.min(1,k/M.supK)}
// the pillars at least 82% of recent event winners have (see the checklist): the model learned from teams that have them,
// so it can't vouch for the upside of a team without them. Same for a weather or terrain the team needs but never sets.
// Each missing piece cuts the above-average edge by 15% (never below 40% of it in all). Oct 27: plans that can't work
// count too, a Trick Room team without slow attackers (30%) and items you picked that never activate (15% each).
const PILLARS={speed:'no speed control',fake:'no lead support (Fake Out, Intimidate or redirection)',spread:'no spread damage',mega:'no Mega'};
function structCredit(ms){if(ms.length!==6)return {c:1,miss:[]};let CK=null;try{CK=proChecks(ms,true)}catch(e){}if(!CK)return {c:1,miss:[]};
  // the Mega rule is fixed (at least one Mega), so the score never depends on your "Megas on team" setting
  const miss=CK.items.filter(x=>PILLARS[x.k]&&x.k!=='mega'&&!x.ok&&!x.skip).map(x=>PILLARS[x.k]);if(!ms.some(m=>m.p&&m.p.m))miss.push(PILLARS.mega);
  let w=.15*miss.length;const add=(t,k)=>{miss.push(t);w+=k};
  // a Pokémon whose ability needs weather or terrain that nothing on the team sets (Swift Swim with no rain) is a broken piece
  try{const fullMs=ms.map(m=>m._mv&&m._mv.length?m:{...m,_mv:memberMoves(m).moves});const gp=gamePlan(fullMs);for(const r of gp.rows)if((r.k==='weather'||r.k==='terrain')&&r.st==='miss')add(String(r.note||(r.k==='weather'?'weather it needs':'terrain it needs')).replace(/[.\s]+$/,''),.15);
    // a Trick Room team (no Tailwind) with at most one slow Pokémon besides its setters. Real Limitless teams built like
    // that won 47.9% of games vs 51.6% with two slow attackers (Oct 27): about half the edge of a strong team, so 30%.
    const tp=trPlan(fullMs);if(tp&&!tp.tw){const att=tp.slow.filter(m=>!tp.set.includes(m));if(att.length<=1)add(`Trick Room with ${att.length?'only one slow attacker ('+att[0].d+')':'no slow attackers'} to use it`,.3)}
    // the Trick Room setter itself holds Choice Scarf: it moves last under its own room and is locked into the move
    if(tp&&tp.scarf.some(m=>tp.set.includes(m)))add(`${tp.scarf.filter(m=>tp.set.includes(m)).map(m=>m.d).join(', ')} sets Trick Room while holding Choice Scarf`,.15);
    // an item you picked that can never work (a Terrain Seed with no terrain, Room Service with no Trick Room)
    // (Choice Scarf on a non-setter is a warning only: real teams with it did no worse, see the setter rule above)
    for(const d of deadItems(fullMs))if(d.k!=='scarftr'&&d.m.heldItem)add(d.why,.15)}catch(e){}
  return {c:Math.max(.4,1-w),miss}}
// Team strength in the model's own units, adjusted exactly as the Team Score adjusts it. Team Score, fix plans, Best
// additions, Build for me and the Gauntlet all read this one number, so they can never disagree about which team is stronger.
// upside needs proof: an untried mix, or a team missing what nearly every winning team has, gets its above-average edge cut
// Every team is read the same way: 4 moves (an opponent's likely-move list keeps its 4 most used), its default item when
// none is set, then Item Clause and dead items (a mirror of your own six in the Gauntlet is exactly even).
function strengthX(names,ms,skipStruct){if(ms){ms=resolveItems(ms.map(m=>{if(!m||!m.p)return m;let o=m;if(o.moves&&o.moves.length>4)o={...o,moves:o.moves.slice(0,4)};if(!o.item&&!o.p.m)o={...o,item:defaultItem(o.p)};return o}));const all=ms;ms=ms.map(m=>m&&m.heldItem&&!itemWorks(all,m.heldItem)?{...m,_deadItem:true}:m)}const mx=META.model.meanX||0,xRaw0=teamX(names,ms),dev=xRaw0-mx;
  const cr=dev>0?mixCredit(names):1,sc=!skipStruct&&dev>0&&ms?structCredit(ms):{c:1,miss:[]};return {xRaw:mx+(dev>0?dev*cr*sc.c:dev),x0:xRaw0,cr,sc}}
// The model is trained on doubles tournament games only.
function winVsField(names,mine,msIn){if(!modelOK()||names.length<6)return null;{const b=names.map(n=>(byName.get(n)||{}).b||n);if(new Set(b).size<b.length)return null}
  const ms=mine?(msIn||members()):null,mx=META.model.meanX||0;
  const {xRaw,x0,cr,sc}=strengthX(names,ms),x=calibK()*(xRaw-mx);
  const pa=mine?personalAdj():null;let xp=null;if(pa){xp=x;for(const n of names)xp+=(pa.get(n)||0)/4}
  return {p:sig(x),pYou:xp==null?null:sig(xp),x:xRaw,pct:strengthPct(xRaw),pctRaw:strengthPct(x0),credit:cr,tested:testedPairs(names),struct:sc,illegal:ms?itemClause(ms):[]}}
function winVs(a,b,ma,mb){if(!modelOK())return null;return sig(calibK()*(strengthX(a,ma).xRaw-strengthX(b,mb).xRaw))}
// where a team's strength falls among real tournament teams from the last 45 days (0-100, from the shipped percentiles)
function strengthPct(x){const d=META.model&&META.model.dist,q=d&&Array.isArray(d.q)&&d.q.length===101?d.q:null;if(!q)return null;
  if(x<=q[0])return 0;if(x>=q[100])return 100;let i=0;while(i<99&&q[i+1]<x)i++;const lo=q[i],hi=q[i+1];return i+(hi>lo?(x-lo)/(hi-lo):0)}
function modelLine(){const m=META.model;if(!m||!m.eval)return '';const e=m.eval,t=e.model_teams_only,r=e.raw_win_rates;
  return `Trained on ${(m.games||0).toLocaleString('en-US')} real games with player skill taken out. ${m.sets&&Object.keys(m.sets).length?'It reads items, Abilities and moves too. ':''}On ${e.test_games.toLocaleString('en-US')} held-out games it called ${(t.acc*100).toFixed(1)}% of winners (raw win rates: ${(r.acc*100).toFixed(1)}%).`}


// ---- team preparation: what every suggestion, build and saved team is judged by (Oct 28) ----
// The win model above stays loaded in the background (research, and the Sleepers same-players test), but it no longer
// scores or ranks teams: predicting who wins from six Pokémon, knowing nothing about the players, isn't something the data
// supports (held-out: the model told top 8 from the rest only 59% of the time, 50% at regionals). Teams are judged by facts
// instead: the share of the top-30 threats they're ready for (55-threats.js), and 0 when the Item Clause makes them illegal.
// tsOf keeps its old name so every caller (Fix plans, Quick add, Best additions, Build for me, saved teams) switches at once.
/* code review A2-M9 (documented, Nov 16): an Item Clause clash makes tsOf 0 on purpose (an illegal team is never counted as strong in the
   background numbers: track record, winners' medians); the Strength Score itself (CH.gradeOf) keeps its number and flags the team
   'Not legal yet', so a player sees the score their six would get once the items are fixed */

// the full report for any six (or fewer), with the items each member would really hold

// speed (Nov 18, pre-launch 2d): tsOf and prepOf for the background planner (yield* them): a pause after each threat, same numbers, same cache

// A team's grade, the big number on My team (readiness + team strength − missed checks), for any six (Nov 7, Sim: "it says +2
// ready but my score went from 93 to 91"). Suggestions are judged and labelled by it, so the card matches what Apply does.
// a member's nature and stat points as a cache-key part (code review A2-I4 + A1-I2, Nov 17: the grade memo and Test Run's cache

function spreadKey(m){const c=m&&m.spread;return c?(c.nat||'')+'/'+[c.hp,c.atk,c.def,c.spa,c.spd,c.spe].join('.'):'c'}
const gradeMemo=new Map();
function gradeTeam(team){if(!team||team.length<6||typeof CH!=='object'||!CH.gradeOf)return null;
  const k=metaVersion+'#'+team.map(m=>m.p.n+'@'+(m.ability||'')+(m.abChosen?'!':'')+'/'+(m.heldItem||'')+(m.itemDefault?'d':'')+':'+(m.custom?'!':'')+((m._mv&&m._mv.length?m._mv:m.moves)||[]).map(x=>x&&x.name).join(',')+'~'+spreadKey(m)).sort().join('|');

    // a six that real players ran and lost with more than the field average is never an upgrade, whatever its grade
    // (Nov 7, Sim: the top suggestion took a 93 (A) to a C) a six that is a real tournament team is graded by its real win rate
    // on My team, so it is judged on that same scale here: 60% wins = 90 (A), 55% = 80 (B), 50% = 70 (C), 45% = 60 (D)
    }catch(e){}
  if(gradeMemo.size>500)gradeMemo.clear();gradeMemo.set(k,g);return g}

function gradeChip(g0,g1,cls){if(g0==null||g1==null)return '';const d=g1-g0;

  return `<span class="${cls||'tsd'} ${d>0?'up':d<0?'dn':''}" title="${tt}">${CH.gradeMove(g0,g1)}</span>`}
