// round words 2: the damage-calc reason in calc shorthand. Only the wording changes; every number, move and condition stays
// ("Mega Charizard Y moves first and OHKOs with Weather Ball, 212–249% of HP" -> "Mega Charizard Y: moves first, OHKO with Weather Ball, 212–249% HP").
// The full sentence stays as the line's title.
function calcShort(w){return String(w||'')
  .replace(/ moves first( \([^)]*\))? and OHKOs with /,(m,t)=>`: moves first${t||''}, OHKO with `)
  .replace(/ survives its (.+?) \(at most (\d+)% of HP\) and OHKOs back with /,': survives $1 (≤$2% HP), OHKO back with ')
  .replace(/ takes at most (\d+)% of HP from (.+?) and 2HKOs with /,': takes ≤$1% HP from $2, 2HKO with ')
  .replace(/ is immune to its attacks and 2HKOs with /,': immune to its attacks, 2HKO with ')
  .replace(/% of HP/g,'% HP').replace(/ \(only if your (.+?) isn't up: leave its setter out\)/,'\nNot in your $1: leave its setter out')}
// What a change does to the team's preparation, threat by threat, in plain words: [{up, html}] (best gains first, then losses)
function threatDiff(baseTeam,team,max=3){const A=prepOf(baseTeam),B=prepOf(team);if(!A||!B||A.rows.length!==B.rows.length)return [];
  const up=[],dn=[];A.rows.forEach((ra,j)=>{const rb=B.rows[j];if(!rb||ra.t.n!==rb.t.n)return;
    if(rb.level>ra.level){const ev=THR.evidence(rb);up.push({w:ra.t.w,html:`<b>Covers ${esc(rb.t.d)}</b>: ${ra.label} → ${rb.label}<span class="thcalc" title="${esc(ev)}">${calcShort(ev).split('\n').map((x,i)=>i?`<span class="thcond">${esc(x)}</span>`:esc(x)).join('')}</span>`})}
    else if(rb.level<ra.level)dn.push({w:ra.t.w,html:`<span class="warnline">Loses ${esc(rb.t.d)}:</span> ${ra.label} → ${rb.label}`})});
  up.sort((x,y)=>y.w-x.w);dn.sort((x,y)=>y.w-x.w);return [...up.slice(0,max).map(x=>({up:true,html:x.html})),...dn.slice(0,max).map(x=>({up:false,html:x.html}))]}// ---------- matchups learned from real games (ctr.mjs, Nov 8): which Pokémon do well against which, once each team's strength
// and each player's skill are taken out. Only used against a known opponent (Battle: Plan a Match); averaged over the whole
// field it added nothing to the grade, so the grade never uses it.
let ctrCache={m:null,ix:null,K:0};
function ctrVal(a,b){const C=META.model&&META.model.ctr;if(!C||!Array.isArray(C.sp)||!Array.isArray(C.v))return null;
  if(ctrCache.m!==C)ctrCache={m:C,ix:new Map(C.sp.map((n,i)=>[n,i])),K:C.sp.length};const {ix,K}=ctrCache,i=ix.get(a),j=ix.get(b);if(i==null||j==null||i===j)return null;
  const lo=Math.min(i,j),hi=Math.max(i,j),k=lo*K-lo*(lo+1)/2+(hi-lo-1),v=(C.v[k]||0)/1000;return i<j?v:-v}
const ONE_GAME_K=.85;
// your six against theirs: a verdict in words, and the pairs that move it most
function realMatch(mine,theirs){if(!modelOK()||!META.model.ctr)return null;const A=mine.map(m=>m.p.n),B=theirs.map(o=>o.p.n);if(A.length<4||B.length<4)return null;
  let mt=0;const pr=[];for(const a of A)for(const b of B){const v=ctrVal(a,b);if(v==null)continue;mt+=v;pr.push({a,b,v})}
  let base=0,full=false;try{if(A.length===6&&B.length===6){base=calibK()*(teamX(A,mine)-teamX(B,null));full=true}}catch(e){}
  // ONE game, not a tournament round (Sim, Nov 8: single games first). The model learned from rounds, 86.5% best of 3. On single
  // games its edges fit 1.02× (5,250 team-sheet games, ±0.26), 0.82× (20,000 ladder games, ±0.35) and 0.6× (best-of-1 rounds,
  // ±0.37); weighted by how sure each is, about 0.85 (docs/deep-check.md).
  const z=ONE_GAME_K*(base+mt),p=sig(z),word=p>=.53?'Favoured':p>.47?'Even match':'Unfavoured';/* the word list (Sim, Nov 9): three words, same as My Team; the old five-step scale's .53/.47 edges kept */
  pr.sort((x,y)=>Math.abs(y.v)-Math.abs(x.v));return {p,word,full,key:pr.filter(x=>Math.abs(x.v)>=.02).slice(0,3),games:META.model.ctr.games||0}}
// ---------- how sure a suggestion is to help (Nov 8, step 6): a swap changes only some Pokémon and pairs, so only their
// uncertainty counts. P(the new six is really stronger) from the change in strength and the spread of the parts that differ
// (model.monSd / pairSd, scaled by SWAP_K: experiments/unsure.mjs found the raw spreads about 30% wide on later results).
// Grade-raising suggestions need 75%+ (a third of strength-raising swaps qualify); fixes for real problems don't.
var SWAP_K=.7,SWAP_MIN=.75;
function swapSure(base,team){const M=META.model;if(!M||!M.monSd||!base||!team||base.length!==6||team.length!==6)return null;
  const vals=Object.values(M.monSd).filter(isFinite);if(!vals.length)return null;
  const A=base.map(m=>m.p.n),B=team.map(m=>m.p.n),sa=new Set(A),sb=new Set(B),mx=Math.max(...vals),p0=+M.pairSd0||.05,pk=(a,b)=>a<b?a+'|'+b:b+'|'+a;
  const pairs=ns=>{const s=new Set();for(let i=0;i<6;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<6;j++)s.add(pk(ns[i],ns[j]));return s},pa=pairs(A),pb=pairs(B);let v=0;
  for(const n of A)if(!sb.has(n))v+=(M.monSd[n]??mx)**2;for(const n of B)if(!sa.has(n))v+=(M.monSd[n]??mx)**2;
  for(const k of pa)if(!pb.has(k))v+=((M.pairSd||{})[k]??p0)**2;for(const k of pb)if(!pa.has(k))v+=((M.pairSd||{})[k]??p0)**2;
  if(!(v>0))return null;let d;try{d=teamX(B,team)-teamX(A,base)}catch(e){return null}
  const z=d/(Math.sqrt(v)*SWAP_K);return 1/(1+Math.exp(-1.702*z))} // normal CDF, logistic approximation
function sureWord(p){return p==null?'':p>=.9?'Likely helps':p>=SWAP_MIN?'Probably helps':''}

=================== FILE app/src/app/58-strength.js (design6)
// ---------- Strength Score: the grade's working-out (R4, Nov 11) ----------
// Moved unchanged from 60-concept-h.js so the rebuild (app/src/v2) uses the same numbers: the score, its band word, how sure it
// is, the likely range, the six's own record, partial teams, the rank among real teams and how many top threats it handles.
// No HTML here. 60-concept-h.js adds the drawing to the same CH object.
const CH = {};
/* GRADE v2 (Nov 7, Sim: "more reflective of the actual team and not have our stats contradicting each other").
   The grade answers one question: how often should this six win? Its strength is the real-games team model (Pokémon, pairs and
   sets, player skill taken out) plus this exact six's own tournament record, also with its players' skill and its opponents'
   strength taken out, and trusted more the more games it has (six.mjs, daily). Tested on later games it had not seen
   (experiments/grade-v2.mjs): it picked the winner in 56.1% of games, against 53.6% for the grade it replaces.
   Shown as the expected win rate against today's tournament field; the number behind the letter is where the six ranks among
   real tournament teams (0-100), and the letter comes from that rank: A top 10%, B next 20%, C middle 40%, D next 20%, F bottom
   10%. How the six handles the top threats is shown on its own, as Matchups: it explains what beats the team, it isn't part of
   the grade (it predicted wins far less well). A team of fewer than six keeps the old check, marked "so far". */
/* STRENGTH SCORE (Sim, Nov 9; docs/strength-score.md). The 0-100 number says how strong the team is altogether, not where it ranks:
   80 + 122 × (chance to beat today's tournament teams − 50%), the chance being CH.fieldWin (player skill taken out, recomputed daily
   from real teams). A team that beats today's tournament teams half the time scores 80; the best 2% of real sheets about 92; no Mega
   lands in the 50s. Same ranking as the old grade (scoreboard Nov 9: identical picks); each 10-point band won more than the one
   below on later games. The rank among real teams is kept as G.rk (small print). Letters: one per 10 points (A 90+, B 80s, C 70s,
   D 60s, F under 60) until the band words replace them (Step B). */
CH.SS_MID = 80; CH.SS_K = 122;
CH.ssOfE = e => e == null || !isFinite(e) ? null : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, CH.SS_MID + CH.SS_K * (e - .5)));
CH.WORDS = [[90, 'Top tier'], [80, 'Tournament ready'], [70, 'Solid'], [60, 'Needs work'], [-1, 'Missing a basic']];
CH.wordOf = s => s == null || !isFinite(s) ? '' : CH.WORDS.find(w => s >= w[0])[1];
CH.wordOfL = L => ({A: 'Top tier', B: 'Tournament ready', C: 'Solid', D: 'Needs work', F: 'Missing a basic'})[String(L || '')[0]] || '';
CH.grade = s => s >= 90 ? 'A' : s >= 80 ? 'B' : s >= 70 ? 'C' : s >= 60 ? 'D' : 'F'; // the band (colours, data-band)
// Nov 9 (Sim chose: plus/minus on every letter, so players feel the difference inside a band): same ranking, finer labels.
// Tested on later games: the top and bottom bands differ clearly; neighbouring steps differ by ~1-2 wins per 100 games, too small to
// confirm with today's data, so a step is a rank (where the team sits), not a promise of more wins. F stays plain.
CH.gradeFull = s => CH.grade(Math.round(s));
CH.gradeOld = s => s >= 90 ? 'A' : s >= 80 ? 'B' : s >= 70 ? 'C' : s >= 60 ? 'D' : 'F';
// this exact six's own record (δ in team-strength units, and its games), from model.sixes
CH.sixOf = ms => { const S = META.model && META.model.sixes; if (!S || !ms || ms.length !== 6) return null; const v = S[ms.map(m => (m.p || m).n).sort().join('|')]; if (!Array.isArray(v)) return null;
  // Deep Check, Nov 8: the record was earned with the sets real players ran, so it only counts for Pokémon on a set like theirs.
  // Sim, Nov 16 (batch 9B): a Pokémon counts as changed when you gave it a move or a held item that real tournament players never
  // ran on it (its tournament set data), so an imported real team sheet keeps its whole record (611 of 709 real sheets measured; the
  // stricter "top 6 moves / top 3 items" took part of the record from 550 of them). A six with every set changed keeps none of it.
  // (Code review A2-I1: this check used to throw on every team and was swallowed, so no edit ever counted.)
  const ran = (L, x) => (L || []).some(r => (Array.isArray(r) ? r[0] : r) === x);
  const off = m => { const st = typeof defSet === 'function' ? defSet(m.p) : null; if (!st) return false;
    const pm = new Set((typeof popularMoves === 'function' ? popularMoves(m.p) : []).map(x => x && (x.name || x)));
    const mv = (m.custom ? (m.moves || []) : []).map(x => x && (x.name || x)).filter(Boolean);
    const mvOff = (st.mv || []).length > 0 && mv.some(x => !pm.has(x) && !ran(st.mv, x));
    const it = m.heldItem && !m.itemDefault && !m.p.m ? (typeof canonItem === 'function' ? canonItem(m.heldItem) : m.heldItem) : null;
    const T = typeof topItems === 'function' ? topItems(m.p) : null, its = [...((T && T.it) || []), ...(st.it || [])].map(r => Array.isArray(r) ? r[0] : r).map(x => typeof canonItem === 'function' ? canonItem(x) : x);
    return mvOff || !!(it && its.length && !its.includes(it)); };
  const mine = ms.filter(m => m && m.p && off(m)).length, share = (6 - mine) / 6; if (!share) return null; /* none of the record is this team's: it reads as our estimate, not 'based on N real games' */
  return {d: (+v[0] || 0) * share, g: +v[1] || 0, share}; };
// how sure the rating is (Nov 8, step 5): the spread of the team's strength from how much real evidence stands behind each of
// its Pokémon and pairs (model.monSd / pairSd), widened by CH.SD_K, the factor that made those spreads match later results
// (experiments/unsure.mjs); a six's own record narrows it. Shown as an 80% range on the 0-100 rating.
CH.SD_K = .7; // experiments/unsure.mjs (Nov 8): the raw spreads were about 30% wider than later results showed
/* Sim, Nov 8: one word instead of a range (an honest range is wide and makes the grade feel vague): High when the team's
   parts are backed by lots of real games (spread ≤ .12, about a well-known six), Medium up to .2, Low beyond. A six with
   100+ games of its own is at least Medium. */
CH.sureOf = (ms, six) => { const sd = CH.sdOf(ms); if (sd == null) return null; let k = sd <= .12 ? 2 : sd <= .2 ? 1 : 0; if (six && six.g >= 100) k = Math.max(k, 1);
  return [{w: 'Low', t: 'few real games back this exact mix, so it could move a lot as results come in'}, {w: 'Medium', t: 'its Pokémon are well known, but this mix of them less so'}, {w: 'High', t: 'its Pokémon and pairings are backed by lots of real games'}][k]; };
CH.sdOf = ms => { const M = META.model; if (!(CH.SD_K > 0) || !M || !M.monSd || !ms || ms.length !== 6) return null; const vals = Object.values(M.monSd).filter(isFinite); if (!vals.length) return null; const ns = ms.map(m => (m.p || m).n), mx = Math.max(...vals), p0 = +M.pairSd0 || .05;
  let v = 0; for (const n of ns) v += (M.monSd[n] ?? mx) ** 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 6; j++) { const k = ns[i] < ns[j] ? ns[i] + '|' + ns[j] : ns[j] + '|' + ns[i]; v += ((M.pairSd || {})[k] ?? p0) ** 2; }
  return Math.sqrt(v) * CH.SD_K; };
CH.rangeOf = (ms, y) => { const sd = CH.sdOf(ms); if (sd == null || y == null) return null; const a = CH.ssOfE(CH.fieldWin(y - 1.2816 * sd)), b = CH.ssOfE(CH.fieldWin(y + 1.2816 * sd));
  return a == null || b == null ? null : [Math.round(a), Math.round(b)]; };
// win chance against today's field: the average over real tournament teams (the shipped percentiles)
CH.fieldWin = y => { const q = META.model && META.model.dist && META.model.dist.q; if (!Array.isArray(q) || q.length !== 101) return null; const c = calibK(); let t = 0; for (const v of q) t += sig(c * (y - v)); return t / q.length; };
// strength, rank among real teams (0-100) and expected win rate, with and without the six's own record
/* Sim, Nov 7: "I don't want it to be the case just because the team isn't a tournament team it gets discredited too much if the
   six Pokémon are all strong picks and cover the meta". Tested on later games (experiments/grade-v2.mjs):
   - matchups (readiness against the top 30: offense and defense calcs) help a little on top of strength, at a small weight
     (more makes predictions worse): CH.MU_K per point of readiness above or below the average real team (79%), about ±0.7 of
     expected win rate between a team that covers the meta like the top tenth and one with the holes of the bottom tenth;
   - the 7 team checks added nothing, so they are checklist advice only, no longer a discount on strength;
   - the caution for sixes whose pairs are rarely seen together made no measurable difference, so it is eased: at most a quarter
     of above-average strength is held back for a never-seen combination (it was half). */
CH.MU_K = .003; CH.MU_AVG = 79;
CH.strengthOf = (ms, ready) => { try { if (!ms || ms.length !== 6 || typeof modelOK !== 'function' || !modelOK()) return null; const names = ms.map(m => m.p.n);
  if (new Set(names.map(n => (byName.get(n) || {}).b || n)).size < 6) return null; const xs = strengthX(names, ms, true); if (!xs || !isFinite(xs.x0)) return null;
  const M = META.model, mx = M.meanX || 0, k = typeof testedPairs === 'function' ? testedPairs(names) : null, cr = k == null || !M.supK ? 1 : .75 + .25 * Math.min(1, k / M.supK);
  const dv = xs.x0 - mx, x = mx + (dv > 0 ? dv * cr : dv), mu = ready == null ? 0 : CH.MU_K * (ready - CH.MU_AVG);
  const six = CH.sixOf(ms), y0 = x + mu, y = y0 + (six ? six.d : 0), pct = strengthPct(y), e = CH.fieldWin(y); if (pct == null || e == null) return null;
  const e0 = CH.fieldWin(x), eMu = CH.fieldWin(y0); return {x, y, six, mu, pct, ss: CH.ssOfE(e), ss0: CH.ssOfE(e0), ssMu: CH.ssOfE(eMu), pct0: strengthPct(x), pMu: strengthPct(y0), e, e0, eMu, rg: CH.rangeOf(ms, y), su: CH.sureOf(ms, six)}; } catch (e) { return null; } };
/* Partial teams on the same scale (Nov 8, foundation plan Phase 1): a team of 1-5 is rated as if each empty slot were an
   average tournament pick, so the number means the same thing as for a full six and each Pokémon you add moves it in a way
   you can follow. Known Pokémon, their pairs and their sets count as for a full team; an empty slot counts 1/6 of an average
   team's strength; matchups and the six's own record wait for all six. Marked "so far" everywhere. CH.PARTIAL_V2=false: old check. */
CH.PARTIAL_V2 = true;
CH.partialOf = ms => { try { if (!ms || !ms.length || ms.length >= 6 || typeof modelOK !== 'function' || !modelOK()) return null; const names = ms.map(m => m.p.n);
  if (new Set(names.map(n => (byName.get(n) || {}).b || n)).size < names.length) return null; const M = META.model, mx = M.meanX || 0;
  const xk = strengthX(names, ms, true).x0; if (!isFinite(xk)) return null; const y = xk + (6 - names.length) / 6 * mx, pct = strengthPct(y), e = CH.fieldWin(y); if (pct == null || e == null) return null;
  const ss = CH.ssOfE(e); return {x: y, y, six: null, mu: 0, pct, ss, ss0: ss, ssMu: ss, pct0: pct, pMu: pct, e, e0: e, eMu: e, rg: null, su: null, partialN: names.length}; } catch (e) { return null; } };
// The letter (Sim, Oct 30): readiness for the top 30 threats, minus 6 points for each of the team checklist's checks it misses.
// Measured on 904 tournament teams with records: each missed check cost about 1% of games won, and 6 points per miss put the
// letters in the same order as the teams' finishes most often. Weather and Taunt/Encore aren't checks: teams without them won
// just as often.
CH.GMISS = 6;
// Real results first (Sim, Oct 30): when these exact six have 30+ tournament games, the letter comes from how often they won.
// On 339 real teams, our own grade didn't line up with their records (higher grades didn't win more), so a real record wins.
// 50% is an average team; each letter is 5 points of win rate. The win rate is already pulled toward the field for small samples.
CH.REC_MIN = 30;
CH.recLetter = wr => wr >= .6 ? 'A' : wr >= .55 ? 'B' : wr >= .5 ? 'C' : wr >= .45 ? 'D' : 'F';
CH._recMap = null; CH._recSrc = null;
// every six with 30+ games (results.records, winners and losers alike); older data only has the winning ones (results.proven)
CH.recList = () => RES && (Array.isArray(RES.records) ? RES.records : Array.isArray(RES.proven) ? RES.proven : null);
CH.recOf = ms => { const L = CH.recList(); if (!ms || ms.length !== 6 || !L) return null;
  if (CH._recSrc !== L) { CH._recSrc = L; CH._recMap = new Map(); for (const [ns, n, wr, g] of L) if (Array.isArray(ns) && ns.length === 6 && g != null && +g >= CH.REC_MIN) CH._recMap.set(ns.slice().sort().join('|'), {n: +n, wr: +wr, g: +g}); }
  return CH._recMap.get(ms.map(m => (m.p || m).n).sort().join('|')) || null; };
// Teams with no record of their own (Sim, Oct 30: "accuracy on point"): the grade adds what the team-strength model (trained on
// 35k real games, player skill taken out) says about these Pokémon and sets. Tested on events neither had seen
// (experiments/grade-eval.mjs): newest 20% of Limitless games, who won: readiness alone 52.5%, model alone 54.8%, grade + 0.75×model
// 55.1%; official RK9 events, top 8 vs the rest: grade 0.586, model 0.515, blend 0.586. So the blend is the best on both. The model
// part is 0.75 × the grade's spread (15 points) per standard deviation of real teams' model strength, centred on the median team.
// Re-tested Oct 31 on cleaned data (themed events removed; 'ready for the top threats' no longer charged twice): blend 55.3% of
// held-out games, 0.576 at official events; the grade's spread is now 12.1 points, so 0.75 of it is 9.1 per model SD.
CH.GMODEL = 9.1;
CH.modelPts = ms => { try { if (!ms || ms.length !== 6 || typeof modelOK !== 'function' || !modelOK()) return null; const q = META.model.dist && META.model.dist.q; if (!Array.isArray(q) || q.length !== 101) return null;
  const sd = (q[84] - q[16]) / 2; if (!(sd > 0)) return null; const x = strengthX(ms.map(m => m.p.n), ms).x0; return isFinite(x) ? Math.max(-25, Math.min(25, CH.GMODEL * (x - q[50]) / sd)) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } };
// 'Ready for the top threats' is itself the readiness score, so it isn't charged again as a missed check (sweep, Oct 31: it
// made a 7-point cliff between 74% and 75% ready). The letter comes from the score as shown, rounded.
/* ONE GRADE (Nov 7, Sim: "why are we not able to make this simple?"). Every team has one grade out of 100, always 0-100, and
   its letter comes only from that number (A 90+, B 80+, C 70+, D 60+, F). It blends our own check with how this exact six did
   in real tournaments, gradually: the more real games, the more they count (151 games: 60% real results, 40% our check; no
   games: our check alone). A real win rate is put on the same scale: 60% wins = 90, 55% = 80, 50% = 70, 45% = 60.
   The real win rate itself is shown as its own line under the grade, never as a second grade. */
CH.REC_K = 100;
CH.recScore = wr => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(70 + (wr - .5) * 200)));
/* Letter buffer (Nov 9, Sim asked): the daily data update moves an unchanged team's grade by ~1-2 points, which with plus/minus
   letters would flip a team sitting on a line (B / B+) with nothing changed. For the player's own teams (My Team and saved teams),
   the letter they last saw is kept until the grade clearly crosses into another letter (by 1.5 points). Editing the team makes a new
   key, so a real change shows at once. Kept in this browser only (a per-viewer convenience); without storage it just shows the plain letter. */
CH.STICKY = 1.5;
CH.letterBounds = L => { const T = [[90, 101, 'A'], [80, 90, 'B'], [70, 80, 'C'], [60, 70, 'D'], [-1, 60, 'F']].find(t => t[2] === L); return T ? [T[0], T[1]] : null; };
CH.teamKey = ms => (ms || []).map(m => [m.p && m.p.n, m.heldItem || '', m.ability || '', (m.moves || []).map(x => x && x.name).join(',')].join('|')).sort().join(';');
CH.ownKeys = () => { const sig = JSON.stringify(S.slots) + '#' + JSON.stringify((S.teams || []).map(t => t.slots)); if (CH._okC && CH._okC.sig === sig) return CH._okC.ks; const ks = new Set(); CH._okC = {sig, ks}; try { const cur = typeof members === 'function' ? members() : []; if (cur.length) ks.add(CH.teamKey(cur));
  for (const t of S.teams || []) { const r = typeof teamRating === 'function' ? teamRating(t.slots) : null; if (r && r.ms && r.ms.length) ks.add(CH.teamKey(r.ms)); } } catch (e) {} return ks; };
CH.sticky = (ms, s) => { const sr = Math.round(s), now = CH.gradeFull(sr); let map; try { map = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('np.gl') || '{}') || {}; } catch (e) { return now; }
  const k = CH.teamKey(ms); if (!k) return now; const prev = map[k]; let out = now;
  if (prev && prev !== now) { const b = CH.letterBounds(prev); if (b && sr >= b[0] - CH.STICKY && sr < b[1] + CH.STICKY) out = prev; }
  if (map[k] !== out && !CH._ownBusy) { CH._ownBusy = true; let own; try { own = CH.ownKeys(); } finally { CH._ownBusy = false; }
    if (own.has(k)) { map[k] = out; const ks = Object.keys(map); if (ks.length > 60) delete map[ks[0]]; try { localStorage.setItem('np.gl', JSON.stringify(map)); } catch (e) {} } }
  return out; };
CH.gradeOf = (RD, CK, ms) => { if (!RD) return null; const miss = CK ? CK.items.filter(x => !x.skip && !x.ok && !x.note && x.k !== 'reach') : [], mp = CH.modelPts(ms), rec = CH.recOf(ms);
  const dupSp = !!(ms && ms.length > 1 && new Set(ms.map(m => (m && m.p && m.p.b) || (m && m.p && m.p.n) || '')).size < ms.length);
  const illegal = dupSp || (typeof itemClash === 'function' && ms ? itemClash(ms).length > 0 : !!(CK && CK.items.some(x => x.k === 'items' && !x.ok))), partial = !ms || ms.length < 6, st = partial ? (CH.PARTIAL_V2 ? CH.partialOf(ms) : null) : CH.strengthOf(ms, RD.score);
  if (st) { const s = Math.round(st.ss * 10) / 10; const L = CH._ownBusy ? CH.gradeFull(s) : CH.sticky(ms, s); return {v: 2, s, letter: L, band: L[0] === 'F' ? 'F' : L[0], word: CH.wordOfL(L), rk: st.pct, e: Math.round(st.e * 1000) / 10, e0: Math.round(st.e0 * 1000) / 10, eMu: Math.round(st.eMu * 1000) / 10, pct0: st.ss0, pMu: st.ssMu, six: st.six, st, r: RD.score, illegal, partial, miss, CK, rec, mp, h: CH.hand(RD)}; }
  // fewer than six (or the model not loaded yet): the old check, marked so far
  if (!isFinite(RD.score)) return null;
  const raw = RD.score - CH.GMISS * miss.length + (mp || 0), clamp = v => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(v))), chk = clamp(raw);
  let s = chk, w = 0, real = null; if (rec) { w = rec.g / (rec.g + CH.REC_K); real = CH.recScore(rec.wr); s = clamp((1 - w) * raw + w * real); }
  return {v: 1, s, letter: CH.gradeOld(s), chk, real, w, r: RD.score, illegal, partial, miss, CK, rec, mp, h: CH.hand(RD)}; };
// the third line under every grade: the real win rate, or that there is none yet
const pct1 = x => Math.round(x * 1000) / 10 + '%';
// v2: where the six ranks among real teams, and what the grade rests on (its own games, or our estimate)
CH.rkOf = G => G.rk != null ? G.rk : CH.rankOfS(G.s);
CH.pctTxt = G => { const r = CH.rankShort(CH.rkOf(G)); return r ? r + ' of tournament teams' : ''; }; /* Sim, Nov 14 (Q2 A): no rank line when the rank is unknown */
CH.pctShort = G => CH.rankShort(CH.rkOf(G));
// a grade number (a rank among real teams) as its expected win rate, for explanations
// a Strength Score as its chance to beat today's tournament teams (%), and as a rank among real teams (0-100)
CH.eOfS = s => s == null || !(META.model && META.model.dist) ? null : Math.round((.5 + (s - CH.SS_MID) / CH.SS_K) * 1000) / 10;
CH.rankOfS = s => { const q = META.model && META.model.dist && META.model.dist.q; if (s == null || !Array.isArray(q) || q.length !== 101) return null; const e = .5 + (s - CH.SS_MID) / CH.SS_K;
  let lo = q[0] - 3, hi = q[100] + 3; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2, f = CH.fieldWin(m); if (f == null) return null; if (f < e) lo = m; else hi = m; } return strengthPct((lo + hi) / 2); };
/* a change, on suggestion cards and after Apply (Sim, Nov 7: "giving win percentage is so weird… seems arbitrary"): the grade
   letter and the team rating, the 0-100 rank among real tournament teams the letter comes from: "Grade C → B · rating 41 → 58",
   or "Grade C · rating 41 → 46" when the letter stays. A tiny win-rate change ("−0.6%") claimed more precision than there is. */
/* Shown as a rank (Sim, Nov 8: "77 for a 9-1 team seems so low"): the 0-100 number is where the six ranks among real tournament
   teams, but "77 / 100" reads like a test score (a C+), so the page says "Top 23%" instead. */
CH.rankShort = s => { if (s == null || !isFinite(s)) return ''; const p = Math.round(s); return p >= 99 ? 'Top 1%' : p >= 50 ? `Top ${100 - p}%` : `Bottom ${Math.max(1, p)}%`; };
/* how many of the top threats the team handles (Handled or Well handled), from the same rows the readiness uses */
CH.hand = RD => RD && Array.isArray(RD.rows) && RD.rows.length ? {x: RD.rows.filter(r => r.level >= 2).length, n: RD.rows.length} : null;
// moved unchanged from 60-concept-h.js (R4, Nov 11: the rebuild's verdict line reads it)
CH.handTxt = (RD, short) => { const h = CH.hand(RD); return !h ? '' : short ? `handles ${h.x} of ${h.n}` : `handles ${h.x} of the top ${h.n} threats`; };

// ---------- matchups and the lead pair (moved unchanged from 60-concept-h.js, R4 Nov 11) ----------
CH.LV = ['No answer', 'Shaky', 'Handles it', 'Beats it'];
// the meta's six: the most-used Pokémon at tournaments, one per species
CH.metaSix = () => { const seen = new Set(), out = []; for (const p of ROSTER.slice().sort((a, b) => U(b) - U(a))) { if (!(U(p) > 0) || seen.has(p.b)) continue; seen.add(p.b); out.push(p); if (out.length === 6) break; } return out; };
// your four vs the meta's six: the Battle mode planner (same as Gauntlet), cached per team
CH._four = {key: null, v: null};
CH.four = ms => { if (!ms || ms.length < 4) return null; const six = CH.metaSix(); if (six.length < 4) return null;
  const key = metaVersion + '|' + six.map(p => p.n).join() + '|' + ms.map(m => m.p.n + '@' + (m.item || '') + ':' + m.moves.map(x => x.name).join(',')).join('|');
  if (CH._four.key === key) return CH._four.v; let v = null;
  try { const useA = $('useAbil') ? $('useAbil').checked : true, r = planMatchup(ms, six.map(oppMember), useA); v = {lead: r.lead.map(i => ms[i].p.n), back: r.back.map(i => ms[i].p.n)}; } catch (e) { v = null; }
  CH._four = {key, v}; return v; };
// your four against any six (the Battle mode planner), cached
CH._fv = {key: null, v: null};
CH.fourVs = (ms, names) => { const key = metaVersion + '|' + names.join() + '|' + ms.map(m => m.p.n + '@' + (m.item || '') + ':' + m.moves.map(x => x.name).join(',')).join('|'); if (CH._fv.key === key) return CH._fv.v; let v = null;
  try { const useA = $('useAbil') ? $('useAbil').checked : true, r = planMatchup(ms, withSheet(names.map(n => oppMember(byName.get(n))), realSheetFor(names, null)), useA); v = {lead: r.lead.map(i => ms[i].p.n), back: r.back.map(i => ms[i].p.n)}; } catch (e) { v = null; }
  CH._fv = {key, v}; return v; };
CH.leadWhy = m => { const mv = new Set(m.moves.map(x => x.name)); if (mv.has('Tailwind')) return 'Tailwind turn 1'; if (mv.has('Trick Room')) return 'Trick Room turn 1'; if (mv.has('Fake Out')) return 'Fake Out pressure';
  if (m.ability === 'Intimidate') return 'Intimidate on entry'; if (mv.has('Follow Me') || mv.has('Rage Powder')) return 'redirects attacks'; return ''; };
// one real KO for a red-pen note: the most-used top threat one of yours OHKOs as its solid answer
CH.koNote = RD => { if (!RD) return null; let best = null;
  for (const r of RD.rows) for (const x of r.solid || []) { const du = x.du; if (du && du.win && du.nM === 1 && du.o && x.m && (!best || r.t.w > best.w)) best = {w: r.t.w, n: x.m.n, txt: `${du.o.name || String(du.o.mv).replace(/ \(.*\)$/, '')} OHKOs ${r.t.d}`}; }
  return best; };
CH.lvCount = RD => [0, 1, 2, 3].map(l => RD ? RD.rows.filter(r => r.level === l).length : 0);
CH.order = RD => RD ? RD.rows.slice().sort((a, b) => a.level - b.level || b.t.w - a.t.w) : [];
/* Threats from real results first (Nov 8 readiness test: the damage-calc verdicts didn't predict who wins, −0.2 ±1.4 pts over
   11,800 tournament games; the matchups learned from real games did). For each threat: how teams with your Pokémon have done
   against teams with it (ctrVal, skill and team strength taken out), summed over your six. The calc reading stays underneath. */
CH.realVs = (t, ms) => { try { if (typeof ctrVal !== 'function') return null; const ns = (ms || members()).map(m => m.p.n); let v = 0, k = 0;
  for (const a of ns) { const c = ctrVal(a, t); if (c != null) { v += c; k++; } } if (!k) return null; const pts = (sig(v) - .5) * 100;
  return pts >= 1.5 ? {w: 'Favoured', c: 'ok', pts} : pts <= -1.5 ? {w: 'Unfavoured', c: 'bad', pts} : {w: 'Even match', c: 'mid', pts}; } catch (e) { return null; } };

// ---------- suggestions: the side options (moved unchanged from CH.sugs in 60-concept-h.js, R4 Nov 11) ----------
// When nothing raises the Strength Score: up to 3 swaps from the planner's "more" list that keep every check and about the same
// grade (at most 2 points less, and matchups at most 2 less), never bringing back a Pokémon you just swapped out with Apply.
CH.sideGrades = (more, g0, base) => { const ok = []; for (const pl of more) { if (ok.length >= 3) break; if (pl.ch.some(x => (swOutNow() || new Set()).has(x.c.p.b))) continue;
      if (pl._ts === undefined && pl.team && pl.team.length === 6) { try { pl._ts = tsOf(pl.team); } catch (e) { pl._ts = null; } }
      const gg = g0 != null ? planGrade(pl) : null; if ((gg != null && gg < g0 - 2) || (base != null && pl._ts != null && pl._ts < base - 2)) continue; ok.push(pl); }
  return ok; };

// moved unchanged from 60-concept-h.js (R4, Nov 11)
/* Strength Score (Nov 9): a change shows in score points, "+7 (76 → 83)", and the band word when it changes */
CH.gradeMove = (g0, g1) => { const r0 = Math.round(g0), r1 = Math.round(g1), d = r1 - r0, w0 = CH.wordOf(r0), w1 = CH.wordOf(r1);
  return `${d > 0 ? '+' + d : d < 0 ? '\u2212' + Math.abs(d) : '\u00b10'} (${r0} \u2192 ${r1})${w0 !== w1 ? ', ' + w1 : ''}`; };
// ---------- text helpers for the score (moved from 60-concept-h.js, Nov 11; the share images and the new build use them) ----------
CH.gradeNum = G => !G ? '' : G.v === 2 ? `${Math.round(G.s)} \u00b7 ${CH.wordOfL(G.letter)}${G.partial ? ' so far' : ''}` : `${G.s} / 100${G.partial ? ' so far' : ''}`;
CH.recLine = (G, short) => !G ? '' : G.v === 2 ? (G.six ? (short ? `${CH.n(G.six.g)} real games` : `Based on ${G.six.g.toLocaleString('en-US')} real games`) : (short ? 'Our estimate' : 'Our estimate, not played in tournaments yet')) : G.rec ? (short ? `${pct1(G.rec.wr)} wins in ${CH.n(G.rec.g)} games` : `Real win rate: ${pct1(G.rec.wr)} in ${G.rec.g.toLocaleString("en-US")} games`) : (short ? 'Our estimate · no tournament games yet' : 'Our estimate · not played in tournaments yet');
CH.readyN = RD => { const h = CH.hand(RD); return h ? `${h.x}/${h.n}` : ''; };
CH.n = n => n.toLocaleString ? n.toLocaleString('en-US') : String(n);
// "Why this score?" as data (R4, Nov 11; today's CH.gradeWhy and the new build both draw it): the Strength Score's three parts,
// which add up to it, and what the number means in real games
// the band key under every "Why this score?" (moved from 60-concept-h.js, Nov 11, shared with the new build)
CH.LETTERS = 'Strength Score: 90+ Top tier · 80s Tournament ready · 70s Solid · 60s Needs work · under 60 Missing a basic. A team that beats today\u2019s tournament teams half the time scores 80.';
CH.WHY_TEST = 'Tested on 6,270 later tournament games: teams scoring 90+ won 58.5%, the 80s 53-54%, the 70s 46-50%, the 60s 38-41%. Every 10 points really means more wins.';
CH.whyData = G => { if (!G || G.v !== 2) return null; const t = Math.round(G.s), a = Math.round(G.pct0), b = Math.round(G.pMu);
  return {s: t, letter: G.letter, word: CH.wordOfL(G.letter), e: Math.round(G.e), rank: CH.pctTxt(G), partial: !!G.partial, k: G.partial ? (G.st && G.st.partialN || 0) : null,
    base: a, mu: b - a, own: t - b, hasSix: !!G.six, ownGames: G.six ? G.six.g : null, ready: G.h ? G.h.x + '/' + G.h.n : '', sure: (G.st && G.st.su) || null, illegal: !!G.illegal}; };

=================== FILE app/src/app/59-matchup-data.js (design6)
// ---------- Matchups: the working-out behind every Matchups view and the threat sheet (R4, Nov 11) ----------
// Moved unchanged out of the drawing code (60-concept-h.js threats10 / scouting sheet, 61-concept-h-pages.js chart,
// 08-render-all.js renderThreatCard, 18-archetypes.js renderThreats) so today's page and the new build draw the same numbers.
// Data only, no HTML. Proof that today's page is unchanged: experiments/same-matchups.mjs.

// one cell of the Top 12 grid: what one of your six does against one threat (colour plus a shape, colour-blind check Nov 1)
CH.LVC = {win: ['var(--chok)', 'answers it', '✓', 'ok'], trade: ['var(--chshaky)', 'partial answer', '~', 'part'], lose: ['var(--chbad)', 'loses 1-on-1', '✗', 'bad'], none: ['var(--line2)', 'no direct matchup', '–', 'none']};
CH.cellOf = (r, m) => { const n = m.p.n, has = a => (a || []).some(x => x.m && x.m.n === n); return has(r.solid) ? 'win' : has(r.soft) ? 'trade' : has(r.losers) ? 'lose' : 'none'; };
// the Top 12 grid's rows: the top k threats by top-cut share, worst first
CH.gridRows = (RD, k = 12) => RD.rows.slice().sort((a, b) => b.t.w - a.t.w).slice(0, k).sort((a, b) => a.level - b.level || b.t.w - a.t.w);
// Top 10: the 10 most common threats (by top-cut share), then the other 20 worst first. Nov 10 (Sim picked option 1): the
// real-games verdict leads, so "worst first" means least favoured in real games (calc breaks ties). RV: each rest row's verdict.
CH.top10 = RD => { const top = RD.rows.slice().sort((a, b) => b.t.w - a.t.w).slice(0, 10), set = new Set(top); let rest = CH.order(RD).filter(r => !set.has(r));
  const RV = new Map(rest.map(r => [r, CH.realVs(r.t.n)])); if (rest.some(r => RV.get(r))) rest = rest.slice().sort((a, b) => ((RV.get(a) || {pts: 0}).pts - (RV.get(b) || {pts: 0}).pts) || (a.level - b.level));
  return {top, rest, RV}; };
// the threat sheet: how many threats need a plan (No answer + Shaky), and up to 5 of the worst
CH.needN = RD => { const c = CH.lvCount(RD); return c[0] + c[1]; };
CH.worst5 = RD => CH.order(RD).filter(r => r.level <= 1).slice(0, 5);

// All 30, "Threat preparation": the four groups (No answer, Shaky, Handles it, Beats it), each worst-first by top-cut share
/* the group names and classes (THR_LV) live in 08-render-all.js */
function thrGroup(RD, lv) { return RD.rows.filter(r => r.level === lv).sort((x, y) => y.t.w - x.t.w); }
// the calc reasons behind one threat's verdict, as {k: 'ok'|'part'|'no'|'t2', text}, then the field and its usual set
function thrReasons(r) { const pc = x => Math.round(x * 100), L = [];
  for (const x of r.solid) L.push({k: 'ok', text: x.why}); for (const x of r.soft) L.push({k: 'part', text: x.why}); for (const x of r.warn) L.push({k: 'no', text: x.why});
  if (r.losers.length) L.push({k: 'no', text: `It beats ${r.losers.map(x => `${x.m.d} (${x.du.d.mv} ${pc(x.du.d.min)}-${pc(x.du.d.pct)}%${x.du.nT === 1 ? ', OHKO' : ', 2HKO'}${x.du.first ? '' : `, speed ${x.du.st} vs ${x.du.sm}`})`).join(', ')} 1-on-1${r.overloaded ? `: only ${r.holders} of your Pokémon hold up against it, so any 4 you bring includes at least 2 it beats` : ''}`});
  if (r.mode) L.push({k: 't2', text: `Judged by its job: ${r.mode.map(x => x.name).join(' and ')}`});
  if (!L.length) L.push({k: 'no', text: 'Nothing on the team beats it 1-on-1 or stops what it does'});
  const f = r.field || {};
  return {list: L, field: `${f.weather ? f.weather : 'no weather'}, ${f.terrain ? f.terrain + ' terrain' : 'no terrain'}`, set: r.t.moves.slice(0, 4).map(m => m.name).join(', ') + (r.t.item ? ' @ ' + r.t.item : '')}; }

// All 30, "Biggest threats": top meta Pokémon that hit several of yours super effectively or that nothing answers, most
// dangerous first (up to 8). null = no meta data yet. Each row: the threat, who it hits, who answers it, the scouting report's
// level for it (null when it is not one of the top 30) and the hardest hit it lands on your six.
function biggestThreats(ms, useA) { const d = metaDoc(); if (!d || !d.top || !d.top.length) return null;
  let RDx = null; try { RDx = prepOf(ms); } catch (e) {} const rdRow = n => { if (!RDx) return null; const b = (byName.get(n) || {}).b; return RDx.rows.find(x => x.t && x.t.n === n) || RDx.rows.find(x => x.t && (byName.get(x.t.n) || {}).b === b) || null; };
  const theirs = d.top.slice(0, 20).map(t => { const p = byName.get(t.n); return p ? oppMember(megaFormFor(p, t)) : null; }).filter(Boolean);
  const h = headToHead(ms, theirs, useA);
  return theirs.map((o, j) => { const hit = ms.map((m, i) => i).filter(i => h.def[i][j] && h.def[i][j].x >= 2);
    const ans = ms.map((m, i) => i).filter(i => h.M[i][j] >= 1);
    return {o, j, hit, ans, danger: hit.length * 2 + (ans.length ? 0 : 3) - ans.length * .5 + viability(o.p) / 50}; })
    .filter(r => r.hit.length >= 2 || !r.ans.length).sort((a, b) => b.danger - a.danger).slice(0, 8)
    .map(r => { const row = rdRow(r.o.p.n); r.name = row ? row.t.d : (byName.get(r.o.p.n) || r.o).d; r.lv = row ? row.level : null;
      r.hard = (() => { try { let b = null; ms.forEach(m => { const x = bestHitOn(r.o.p, r.o.moves || [], m.p, null, m); if (x && (!b || x.r.max / x.r.hp > b.x.r.max / b.x.r.hp)) b = {x, m}; });
        return b ? {mv: b.x.mv.name, pct: Math.round(100 * b.x.r.max / b.x.r.hp), m: b.m} : null; } catch (e) { return null; } })();
      return r; }); }

// the threat sheet's lines (moved unchanged from 60-concept-h.js, Nov 11): what's behind the Strength Score as parts that add up
// to it, and how many top threats the team handles
CH.gradeParts = (G, RD) => { const nm = G.miss ? G.miss.length : 0, chk = G.chk != null ? G.chk : G.s; return {nm, ts: chk - RD.score + CH.GMISS * nm, ra: G.s - chk}; };
CH.gradeSum = (G, RD) => { const sg = v => v < 0 ? '−' : '+';
  /* the parts in rating points (Sim, Nov 7: no tiny win-rate pieces): where its Pokémon, pairs and sets alone would rank it, what
     its matchups move, what its own games move */
  if (G.v === 2) { const a = Math.round(G.pct0), b = Math.round(G.pMu), t = Math.round(G.s), m = b - a, r = t - b;
    return `<b>Strength Score <span data-band="${G.band || String(G.letter)[0]}">${t}</span></b> = ${a} from its Pokémon, pairs and sets${m ? ` ${sg(m)} ${Math.abs(m)} for its matchups (ready for ${G.h ? G.h.x + '/' + G.h.n : ''} top threats)` : ''}${G.six && r ? ` ${sg(r)} ${Math.abs(r)} from this six's own ${G.six.g.toLocaleString('en-US')} real games (players' skill taken out)` : ''}${typeof tip === 'function' ? tip('numbers') : ''}`; }
  const {nm, ts, ra} = CH.gradeParts(G, RD);
  return `<b>Grade <span data-band="${G.band || String(G.letter)[0]}">${G.s}</span></b> = ${RD.score} ready${ts ? ` ${sg(ts)} ${Math.abs(ts)} team strength` : ''}${nm ? ` − ${CH.GMISS * nm} (${nm} missed check${nm === 1 ? '' : 's'})` : ''}${G.rec && ra ? ` ${sg(ra)} ${Math.abs(ra)} real results` : ''}${typeof tip === 'function' ? tip('numbers') : ''}`; };
CH.readyTxt = RD => { const h = CH.hand(RD); return h ? `Ready for ${h.x}/${h.n} top threats` : ''; };
CH.handTxt = (RD, short) => { const h = CH.hand(RD); return !h ? '' : short ? `handles ${h.x} of ${h.n}` : `handles ${h.x} of the top ${h.n} threats`; };
CH.LEGEND = 'Strength Score = how strong the team is altogether, from how its Pokémon, pairs and sets do in real games, its matchups against the top threats, and its own record (players\' skill taken out). 90+ Top tier · 80s Tournament ready · 70s Solid · 60s Needs work · under 60 Missing a basic.';

=================== FILE app/src/app/36-lanes.js (design6)
/* ===== Two kinds of suggestions (focus plan Part 3, f3-lanes / f3-none, Nov 10) =====
   Measured before writing a line (Sim: honest numbers that still feel like real upgrades):
   - Real problems are big. On 10,575 real team sheets and 38,513 games, player skill taken out (experiments/lane-fixes.mjs):
     no Mega −23 win-chance points (−37 to −16); Protect on 0-1 of the six −15 (−21 to −10), on 2 −4 (−8 to −2), vs the usual 3.
     Ready for the top threats +3.5 (2.4 to 4.7, checks-real.mjs). Choice Scarf on a Trick Room team: no measurable harm (+0.9,
     −0.9 to +2.7), so it is not pushed as a fix.
   - Plain swaps are small. On real one-swap pairs judged on games the evidence never saw (experiments/lane-test.mjs), the model's
     value gap picks the better six 55% (57% when the gap is 4+ points) and really delivers about 0.35 of what it promises; the
     six's own record picks 50%, a coin flip, so real teams' records can't back a swap on their own.
   So: a FIX repairs a real problem and says what that problem costs in real games; a SWAP is shown only when the model's gain is
   4+ win-chance points, and says about a third of it, the size real swaps deliver; anything else is an OPTION (kept for the
   team checks and game-plan notes, said plainly that real games show no clear edge). No fix and no swap: "Nothing to fix". */
const LANE = {
  PTS: {mega: 23, prot1: 15, prot2: 4, reach: 3.5}, // win-chance points at an even game (see above)
  SWAP_PTS: 4,  // the model gain a plain swap needs (lane-test.mjs: picks the better real six 57.3% at 4+)
  REAL: .35,    // what a model-picked swap really delivers per point it promises (lane-test.mjs slope)
  PROT: new Set(['Protect', 'Detect', 'Spiky Shield', "King's Shield", 'Baneful Bunker', 'Silk Trap', 'Burning Bulwark', 'Obstruct']),
  // the moves a member really runs: its own (custom) moves, else its popular set
  moves(m) { const mv = m && m.moves && m.moves.length ? m.moves : m && m.p ? popularMoves(m.p) : []; return (mv || []).map(x => x && (x.name || x)).filter(Boolean); },
  protCount(ms) { return ms.filter(m => LANE.moves(m).slice(0, 4).some(n => LANE.PROT.has(n))).length; },
  // win-chance points the Strength Score promises for a plan (the score is win chance against today's tournament teams)
  gain(pl, g0) { const g1 = typeof planGrade === 'function' ? planGrade(pl) : null; if (g0 == null || g1 == null) return null; const a = CH.eOfS(g0), b = CH.eOfS(g1); return a == null || b == null ? null : b - a; },
  of(pl, ms, g0) { if (pl._lane) return pl._lane; const fixes = [];
    try { if (itemClash(pl.team).length < itemClash(ms).length) fixes.push({k: 'clause'}); } catch (e) {}
    if (pl.team.length === 6 && !ms.some(m => m.mega) && pl.team.some(m => m.mega)) fixes.push({k: 'mega', pts: LANE.PTS.mega});
    try { const r = t => ((proChecks(t, true) || {items: []}).items.find(x => x.k === 'reach') || {}).ok; if (pl.team.length === 6 && r(ms) === false && r(pl.team)) fixes.push({k: 'reach', pts: LANE.PTS.reach}); } catch (e) {}
    const gain = LANE.gain(pl, g0);
    return pl._lane = fixes.length ? {k: 'fix', fixes, gain} : gain != null && gain >= LANE.SWAP_PTS ? {k: 'swap', gain, real: Math.max(1, Math.round(gain * LANE.REAL))} : {k: 'option', gain}; },
  // after the planner: fixes first, then swaps that clear the bar, then options that cover a check or note; plain swaps below the
  // bar move to the front of More ideas (still one tap away, never removed)
  apply(out, ms, g0) { if (!out || !ms || ms.length !== 6) return out; const keep = [], moved = [];
    for (const pl of out) { const L = LANE.of(pl, ms, g0); const forced = pl.gap && ((pl.gap.fix && pl.gap.fix.length) || (pl.gap.why || []).some(w => /^Meets the /.test(w)));
      if (L.k === 'fix' || L.k === 'swap' || forced) keep.push(pl); else moved.push(pl); }
    const rank = pl => pl._lane.k === 'fix' ? 0 : pl._lane.k === 'swap' ? 1 : 2; keep.sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b));
    out.length = 0; out.push(...keep); if (Array.isArray(out.more)) out.more.unshift(...moved.filter(pl => !out.more.includes(pl)));
    out.nothing = !keep.some(pl => pl._lane.k !== 'option') && !LANE.protFix(ms); return out; },
  // the line under a suggestion: what kind it is, and its honest size in real games
  // the same line as data, {k: 'fix' | 'swap' | 'opt', tag, text}, for the rebuild's own markup (R4, Nov 11); null when the plan has no lane
  lineParts(pl) { const x = LANE.lineParts0(pl); if (!x) return null; let w = ''; try { w = terrainLossLine(pl); } catch (e) {} if (w) x.warn = w; return x; },
  lineParts0(pl) { const L = pl && pl._lane; if (!L) return null;
    if (L.k === 'fix') { const f = L.fixes[0], w = {clause: 'two of your Pokémon hold the same item, so the team can’t be registered; this frees it',
        mega: `adds a Mega: teams with no Mega won about ${L.fixes[0].pts} fewer games in 100`, reach: `answers more of the top threats (Matchups): teams that answer them win about ${Math.round(LANE.PTS.reach)} more games in 100`}[f.k];
      return {k: 'fix', tag: 'Fix', text: w}; }
    if (L.k === 'swap') return {k: 'swap', tag: 'Small edge', text: `about ${L.real} more win${L.real === 1 ? "" : "s"} in 100 games in real play`};
    // audit Nov 19 #3: a plan that fills an empty spot (a team of 1-5) is not a sideways swap: the Option words below compare one six with
    // another ("can't tell it apart from what you have", "no clear edge either way"), which is untrue of adding a Pokémon. No line until
    // Sim picks its words (a Fix line, e.g. "adds a Mega", stays: it is true of the six it makes). W1a (Sim, Nov 19): 'Add · fills your empty spot'
    if ((pl.ch || []).some(x => x && !x.out)) return {k: 'add', tag: 'Add', text: 'fills your empty spot'};
    const note = pl.gap && ((pl.gap.fix && pl.gap.fix.length) || (pl.gap.why || []).some(w => /^Meets the /.test(w)));
    return note ? {k: 'opt', tag: 'Option', text: 'covers a team note; real games show no clear edge either way'}
      : {k: 'opt', tag: 'Option', text: 'a small change: real games can’t tell it apart from what you have'}; },
  // plus a warning when the plan swaps out a terrain setter a teammate's move needs (terrainLoss, 29-fix-planner.js; Sim, Nov 18)
  line(pl) { const x = LANE.lineParts(pl); if (!x) return ''; const b = `<span class="chlane ln-${x.k}"><b>${x.tag}</b> ${x.text}</span>`; return x.warn ? `${b} <span class="chtloss">${x.warn}</span>` : b; },
  // too little Protect: a set fix, not a swap. Members whose popular set runs Protect but whose moves here don't.
  protFix(ms) { if (!ms || ms.length !== 6) return null; const n = LANE.protCount(ms); if (n > 2) return null;
    const who = ms.filter(m => !LANE.moves(m).slice(0, 4).some(x => LANE.PROT.has(x)) && (popularMoves(m.p) || []).slice(0, 4).some(x => LANE.PROT.has(x && (x.name || x))));
    return who.length ? {n, who: who.slice(0, 3 - n), pts: n <= 1 ? LANE.PTS.prot1 : LANE.PTS.prot2} : null; },
  // the card's words (shared with the rebuild, R4 Nov 11): the problem and what it costs, and who should run it
  protWords(f) { return {fix: `only ${f.n} of your six run${f.n === 1 ? 's' : ''} Protect: teams with ${f.n <= 1 ? 'one or none' : 'two'} won about ${f.pts} fewer games in 100 than teams with three`, who: 'Their usual sets run it:', btn: 'Add Protect'}; },
  protCard(ms) { const f = LANE.protFix(ms); if (!f) return ''; const W = LANE.protWords(f);
    return `<div class="chlanecard fix"><p><span class="chlane ln-fix"><b>Fix</b> ${W.fix}</span></p>
      <p class="t2">${W.who} ${f.who.map(m => `<b>${esc(m.d)}</b>`).join(' and ')}.</p><button type="button" class="chghost" data-protfix>${W.btn}</button></div>`; },
  // the move swapped out for Protect: the one its popular set runs least (else the last one)
  protApply(ms) { const f = LANE.protFix(ms); if (!f) return; keepUndo(); /* known-bug SUGG-07b (Nov 17): the toast offered Undo with nothing saved */ for (const m of f.who) { const s = S.slots[m.i]; if (!s) continue;
      const pop = (popularMoves(m.p) || []).map(x => x && (x.name || x)), cur = LANE.moves(m).slice(0, 4); while (cur.length < 4) cur.push('');
      let j = -1, worst = -1; cur.forEach((n, k) => { const r = n ? pop.indexOf(n) : 99; const rr = r < 0 ? 98 : r; if (rr > worst) { worst = rr; j = k; } });
      if (j < 0) j = 3; cur[j] = 'Protect'; s.mv = s.mv || {}; s.mv[S.format] = cur; }
    persist(); render(); try { toast('Protect added: it’s in each set now, change it any time', true); } catch (e) {} },
  // nothing to fix: say so, and point to where this team still has a measured edge
  // its words, shared with the rebuild (R4, Nov 11)
  NONE: {head: 'Nothing to fix.', body: 'Your team has every basic and no swap clearly beats it in real games.', edge: 'Your edge now comes from how you play it: plan each matchup in Battle, and tune your sets.'},
  noneCard() { return `<div class="chlanecard none"><p><b>${LANE.NONE.head}</b> ${LANE.NONE.body}</p>
    <p class="t2">${LANE.NONE.edge}</p>
    <div class="chlanebtns"><button type="button" class="chghost" data-view="matchup">Battle</button><button type="button" class="chghost" data-view="sets">Sets</button></div></div>`; }
};
document.addEventListener('click', e => { const b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-protfix]'); if (!b) return; try { LANE.protApply(members()); } catch (x) {} });

=================== FILE app/src/app/60-concept-h.js (design6)
// ---------- Concept H: Team Preview × Riso Zine (Oct 28, approved with Warm charcoal dark mode) ----------
// Home and Team in the new look. Everything on screen comes from the same data as before (readiness from the threat
// engine, the bring-four planner from Battle mode, events, Showdown replays); nothing is invented. Older pages keep their
// layout and pick up the palette through the shared tokens (concept-h.css). Every feature stays reachable: older Home
// sections and the full scouting report sit in folds that open with the logo's chevron pair (up = closed, down = open).
// CH is created in 58-strength.js (the Strength Score logic, shared with the rebuild)
/* the data behind a number, one shared helper (36-confidence.js) */
CH.basis = basis; CH.thin = thinFor; CH.basisText = basisText;
CH.chev = (x, y, c) => { const p = (dy, col) => `<path d="M${x + 2} ${y + 22 + dy} L${x + 18} ${y + 8 + dy} L${x + 34} ${y + 22 + dy}" fill="none" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`; return p(3, '#FFE800') + p(0, c) + p(13, '#FFE800') + p(10, c); };
// the logo: two +2 chevrons (blue = you, red = them) over the back two slots
CH.logo = (s = 34, tile = '#1D2140') => `<svg width="${s}" height="${s}" viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100" height="100" rx="24" fill="${tile}"/>${CH.chev(12, 22, '#0078BF')}${CH.chev(52, 22, '#FF52B9')}<rect x="20" y="64" width="22" height="18" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#F1E9DA" stroke-width="6"/><rect x="58" y="64" width="22" height="18" rx="6" fill="#F1E9DA"/></svg>`;
// the final logo's two chevrons (blue under red): up = closed, flipped = open. Exactly two chevrons, always.
CH.chev2 = (cls = '') => `<svg class="`+cls+`" viewBox="124 72 264 293" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M144 284.64L256 172.64L368 284.64L368 345.12L256 233.12L144 345.12Z" fill="var(--chblue,#0078BF)"/><path d="M144 204L256 92L368 204L368 264.48L256 152.48L144 264.48Z" fill="var(--chred,#FF48B0)"/></svg>`;
CH.tog = () => `<span class="chtog" aria-hidden="true">${CH.chev2()}</span>`;
// the logo: full lockup (Home), compact header lockup (top bar, menu, footer); light and Warm charcoal
// each copy gets its own mask ids: a hidden copy (the closed menu) would otherwise break the mask for every other copy
CH._lk = 0; CH.uniq = svg => { const n = ++CH._lk; return svg.replace(/id="(np[^"]+)"/g, (m, id) => `id="${id}-${n}"`).replace(/url\(#(np[^)]+)\)/g, (m, id) => `url(#${id}-${n})`); };
/* npLogoSvg (the logo for share images) lives in 44-share-images.js (Nov 11) */
CH.lockup = k => `<span class="nplk ${k}" role="img" aria-label="NastyPlot"><span class="lt">${CH.uniq(NP_SVG[k + 'L'])}</span><span class="dk">${CH.uniq(NP_SVG[k + 'D'])}</span></span>`;
// readiness grade: preparation for the top 30 threats, never a win chance
/* CH.gradeNum lives in 58-strength.js (Nov 11) */
/* CH.recLine lives in 58-strength.js (Nov 11) */
// What's behind the grade, as parts that add up to it: readiness, team strength (the remainder, so the sum is exact), missed
// checks, and real results (how far the real win rate moved it).
/* CH.gradeParts and CH.gradeSum live in 59-matchup-data.js (Nov 11: the threat sheet on the new build shows the same sum) */
/* the same sum, short, for cards and table cells: "92 = 85 ready + 7 strength − 6 checks" */
CH.gradeSumS = (G, RD) => { const sg = v => v < 0 ? '−' : '+';
  if (G.v === 2) { const a = Math.round(G.pct0), b = Math.round(G.pMu), t = Math.round(G.s), m = b - a, r = t - b; return `Rating ${t} = ${a} from its Pokémon and sets${m ? ` ${sg(m)} ${Math.abs(m)} matchups` : ''}${G.six && r ? ` ${sg(r)} ${Math.abs(r)} from its own games` : ''}`; }
  const {nm, ts, ra} = CH.gradeParts(G, RD);
  return `${G.s} = ${RD.score} ready${ts ? ` ${sg(ts)} ${Math.abs(ts)} strength` : ''}${nm ? ` − ${CH.GMISS * nm} for ${nm} missed check${nm === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : ''}${G.rec && ra ? ` ${sg(ra)} ${Math.abs(ra)} real results` : ''}`; };
/* grade letters in the rating band colours (A = the A colour, ...), one source: LTIERS */
/* band colours follow the meaning (Sim, Nov 9): blue and purple for the top bands, neutral for Solid, amber for Needs work, muted
   grey-brown for Missing a basic; never red (red is for opponents). Hex, so the share image (canvas) can use them; each is 3:1+ on
   the light and dark page (large text). */
CH.GCOL = {A: '#0078BF', B: '#8b4fe0', C: '#77808f', D: '#b8740a', F: '#8d7b68'};
CH.gcol = L => CH.GCOL[String(L || '')[0]] || '#8a90a6';
/* Sim, Nov 9: "a team getting a 90 then seeing 85% ready ... is contradictory". Readiness shows as a count everywhere: "Ready for
   24/30 top threats" (Handles it or Beats it); the weighted % stays inside the calculations only. */
/* CH.readyN lives in 58-strength.js (Nov 11) */
/* CH.readyTxt and CH.handTxt live in 59-matchup-data.js (Nov 11) */
/* a team's grade as a badge (Build me a team, Saved teams): the letter in its band colour, the grade number, and the sum
   underneath so "% ready" always reads as one part of the grade. A team with 30+ real games shows its record instead. */
CH.gradeBadge = (G, RD) => { if (!G || !RD) return '';
  /* the same three lines as My team: the letter, the grade out of 100, the real win rate (or none yet); the sum is the tooltip */
  const tt = G.v === 2 ? `Strength Score ${Math.round(G.s)}, ${CH.wordOfL(G.letter)}: ${CH.gradeSumS(G, RD)}. ${CH.pctTxt(G) ? CH.pctTxt(G) + '. ' : ''}${CH.recLine(G)}.` : `Grade ${G.s} (${G.letter}) = ${CH.gradeSumS(G, RD)}. ${CH.recLine(G)}.`;
  /* plain words (Nov 9, Sim: no extra numbers next to the letter): the letter only; the rank and the record are in the hover text */
  return `<span class="npgb" style="--gc:${CH.gcol(G.letter)}" title="${esc(G.v === 2 ? tt : `${CH.gradeNum(G)}. ${tt}`)}"><b class="npgl">${G.v === 2 ? Math.round(G.s) : G.letter}</b></span>`; };
/* round words (Sim, Nov 4): the record and readiness as labelled number cells instead of a sentence, each figure once.
   Record row only with 30+ real games (the grade then comes from it); "Our check" is NastyPlot's own readiness check. */
CH.recStats = (G, RD, hd, c) => { const cell = (v, l, t) => `<span class="wdc"><b>${v}</b><small>${l}${t || ''}</small></span>`;
  const tp = k => typeof tip === 'function' ? tip(k) : '';
  const rec = '';
  const sum = G && !G.partial ? `<div class="wdsum">${CH.gradeSum(G, RD)}</div>` : '';
  const ready = sum ? '' : cell(CH.readyN(RD), 'ready', tp('readiness'));
  const chk = `<div class="wdrow" role="group" aria-label="Our check against the top ${hd ? hd.n : 30} threats"><span class="wdl">Our check, top ${hd ? hd.n : 30} threats${G && G.partial ? tp('numbers') : ''}</span>${ready}${hd ? cell(hd.x, 'handled') : ''}${c[1] ? cell(c[1], 'shaky') : ''}${c[0] ? cell(c[0], 'no answer') : ''}</div>`;
  return `<div class="rd wdstats">${sum}${rec}${chk}</div>`; };
// CH.LETTERS moved to 58-strength.js (R4, Nov 11) so the new build shares it
CH.gradeWhy = (G, o) => { if (!G) return ''; const n = G.CK ? G.CK.n : 0;
  if (G.v === 2) { const W = CH.whyData(G), L2 = `<b class="npgw" style="color:${CH.gcol(W.letter)}">${W.s} \u00b7 ${W.word}</b>`, a = W.base, m = W.mu, r = W.own;
    const su = W.sure, top = `${L2}. Teams scoring like this win about ${W.e}% against today\u2019s tournament teams (${W.rank.replace(/^Top/, 'top').replace(/^Bottom/, 'bottom')} by rank).`;
    /* short (Sim, Nov 7: "just what they should and need to"): what the number means, the three things it's made of, the letters */
    const sg = v => (v > 0 ? '+' : v < 0 ? '−' : '±') + Math.abs(v);
    if (W.partial) { const k = W.k, e = 6 - k;
      return `<div class="chgwhy" id="chGwhy">${L2} so far, with ${k} of 6 picked.<ul class="chgwl"><li>From how your ${k} Pokémon, their pairs and their sets do in real tournament games</li><li>Each of the ${e} empty slot${e === 1 ? '' : 's'} counts as an average tournament pick, so a strong pick raises it and a weak one lowers it</li><li>Matchups and real results join once all six are in</li></ul>${W.illegal ? 'Not legal yet: fix the items first. ' : ''}<span class="t2">${CH.LETTERS}</span></div>`; }
    return `<div class="chgwhy" id="chGwhy">${top}<ul class="chgwl"><li><b>${a}</b> from how its Pokémon, pairs and sets do in real tournament games</li>`
      + `<li><b>${sg(m)}</b> for its matchups (ready for ${W.ready} top threats). They count a little, since players often play around a shaky matchup</li>`
      + (W.hasSix ? `<li><b>${sg(r)}</b> from this exact six's own ${W.ownGames.toLocaleString('en-US')} real games, with its players' skill taken out</li>` : `<li>This exact six hasn’t been played in tournaments yet, so the rest is our estimate</li>`)
      + `</ul><p class="chsure">${CH.WHY_TEST}</p>${su ? `<p class="chsure">How sure: <b>${su.w}</b>. ${su.t[0].toUpperCase() + su.t.slice(1)}.</p>` : ''}${W.illegal ? 'Not legal yet: fix the items first. ' : ''}<span class="t2">${CH.LETTERS}</span>${W.illegal && typeof regNote === 'function' ? regNote() : ''}</div>`; }
  const L = `<b class="npgw" style="color:${CH.gcol(G.letter)}">${G.letter}</b>`, hd = G.h ? ` (handles ${G.h.x} of ${G.h.n})` : '', mp = G.mp != null ? Math.round(G.mp) : null;
  const realTxt = G.rec ? ` These exact six also won ${pct1(G.rec.wr)} of ${G.rec.g.toLocaleString('en-US')} real tournament games (${G.rec.n} player${G.rec.n === 1 ? '' : 's'}), which counts as ${G.real}; with that many games, real results make up ${Math.round(G.w * 100)}% of the grade (more games, more weight).` : ' This exact six hasn\u2019t been played in tournaments yet, so the grade is our own check alone.';
  if (o && o.short) return `<div class="chgwhy" id="chGwhy">${L} · ${G.s} out of 100.${realTxt}<br><span class="t2">A 90+, B 80+, C 70+, D 60+.</span></div>`;
  return `<div class="chgwhy" id="chGwhy">${L} · ${G.s} out of 100${G.partial ? ' so far' : ''}. Our own check starts from ${G.r}% ready for the top 30 threats${hd}${G.CK ? `; ${n - G.miss.length}/${n} team checks` : ''}.`
    + (G.miss.length ? ` Each missed check takes ${CH.GMISS} points off: ${G.miss.map(x => esc(x.label.toLowerCase())).join(', ')}.` : ' No missed checks, so nothing comes off.')
    + (mp != null ? ` ${mp === 0 ? 'Teams with these Pokémon and sets do about average in real games (±0).' : `Teams with these Pokémon and sets do ${mp > 0 ? 'better' : 'worse'} than average in real games (${mp > 0 ? '+' : '−'}${Math.abs(mp)}).`}` : '')
    + realTxt + ` A 90+, B 80+, C 70+, D 60+.${G.partial ? ' The grade settles once all six are in.' : ''}${G.illegal ? ' Not legal yet: fix the items first.' : ''}<br><span class="t2">Weather and Taunt or Encore aren't counted: many event winners run neither, and teams without them win just as often.</span>${G.illegal && typeof regNote === 'function' ? regNote() : ''}</div>`; };
document.addEventListener('click', e => { const b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-chgrade]'); if (!b) return; ui.gwhy = !ui.gwhy; const w = $('chGwhy'); if (w) w.hidden = !ui.gwhy; b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', ui.gwhy ? 'true' : 'false'); });
/* CH.LEGEND lives in 59-matchup-data.js (Nov 11) */
// Motion (Nov 1): when the grade first shows or changes, the ring pops in and the "% ready" counts up from the last number,
// 200 ms, transform/opacity only for the ring; the number is tabular so nothing moves. Off with reduced motion, and only on
// the page that's showing (redraws with the same grade do nothing).
CH.reduced = () => { try { return matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; } catch (e) { return false; } };
/* Nov 2: the headline number is now the grade ("Grade 92"; "85% ready" while the team is still partial): that number counts up,
   and the ring's own number (the same grade) counts with it */
CH.gradeMotion = (head, G, RD) => { const b = head.querySelector('.rd > b'), m = b && /^(\D*)(\d+)(\D*)$/.exec(b.textContent), to = m ? +m[2] : null;
  const key = G ? G.letter + '|' + (to == null ? RD.score : to) : '', prev = CH.gKey; CH.gKey = key;
  if (!G || key === prev || CH.reduced() || head.closest('[hidden]')) return;
  const ring = head.querySelector('.chring'); if (ring && (!prev || prev.split('|')[0] !== G.letter)) ring.classList.add('np-pop');
  const from = prev ? +prev.split('|')[1] : Math.max(0, to - 30); if (to == null || from === to) return;
  const sm = ring && ring.querySelector('small'), smOn = sm && sm.textContent === String(to);
  b.classList.add('np-num'); const put = v => { b.textContent = m[1] + v + m[3]; if (smOn) sm.textContent = String(v); };
  const t0 = performance.now(), tick = t => { const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / 200), v = Math.round(from + (to - from) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3)));
    if (!b.isConnected) return; put(v); if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick); }; put(from); requestAnimationFrame(tick); };
/* CH.n lives in 58-strength.js (Nov 11) */
// a fold: remembers open/closed per device
CH.fold = (key, title, sub, body, open) => { const o = LS.get('cc2.chf.' + key, null); const isOpen = o == null ? !!open : !!o;
  return `<details class="chsec chfold" data-chf="${key}"${isOpen ? ' open' : ''}><summary>${CH.tog()}<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="cht">${title}</div>${sub ? `<div class="chs">${sub}</div>` : ''}</div></summary><div class="chbody">${body}</div></details>`; };
document.addEventListener('toggle', e => { const d = e.target; if (d && d.dataset && d.dataset.chf) LS.set('cc2.chf.' + d.dataset.chf, d.open ? 1 : 0); }, true);
CH.tiles = (RD, attr) => `<div class="chtiles" role="group" aria-label="Top 30 threats, worst first">${CH.order(RD).map(r => { const p = byName.get(r.t.n); return `<button type="button" class="chtile l${r.level}" ${attr}="${esc(r.t.n)}" aria-label="${esc(r.t.d)}: ${CH.LV[r.level]}" title="${esc(r.t.d)}: ${CH.LV[r.level]}">${sprite(p) || `<span class="chk">${esc(r.t.d.slice(0, 3))}</span>`}</button>`; }).join('')}</div>`;
// the 10 most common threats by name (sprite, name, how ready you are), then the other 20 as a small strip, worst first
/* which 10, and the order of the other 20, are worked out in 59-matchup-data.js (CH.top10), shared with the new build */
CH.threats10 = (RD, attr) => { const {top, rest, RV} = CH.top10(RD);
  const rvWord = r => { const R = RV.get(r); return R ? `${R.w}, calc ${CH.LV[r.level]}` : CH.LV[r.level]; };
  const lv = ['bad', 'shaky', 'mid', 'ok'];
  return `${typeof ctrVal === 'function' && META.model && META.model.ctr ? '<p class="t2 chthrsrc">Verdicts from real tournament games: how teams with your Pokémon did against teams with each threat. Calc readings are underneath.</p>' : ''}<div class="chtl10${ui.tlMore ? ' is-more' : ''}">${top.map((r, i) => { const p = byName.get(r.t.n); return `<button type="button" class="chtr l${r.level}${p ? ' tyx' : ''}"${p ? ` style="${tyVars(p)}"` : ''} ${attr}="${esc(r.t.n)}" aria-label="${esc(r.t.d)}: ${CH.LV[r.level]}"><span class="rk">${i + 1}</span>${sprite(p) || ''}<span class="tx"><b>${escName(r.t.d)}</b>${(() => { const R = CH.realVs(r.t.n); return R ? `<span class="st ${R.c}" title="From real tournament games: how teams with your Pokémon did against teams with ${esc(r.t.d)}">${R.w}</span><span class="st chcalcv">Calc: ${CH.LV[r.level]}</span>` : `<span class="st ${lv[r.level]}">${CH.LV[r.level]}</span>`; })()}</span></button>`; }).join('')}</div>
    ${top.length > 5 ? `<button type="button" class="chtlmore" data-tlmore="1" aria-expanded="${ui.tlMore ? 'true' : 'false'}">${ui.tlMore ? 'Show top 5 only' : `Show ${top.length - 5} more`}</button>` : ''}
    ${rest.length ? `<div class="chk chtk">The next ${rest.length}, worst first</div><div class="chtiles chsm" role="group" aria-label="The next ${rest.length} threats">${rest.map(r => { const p = byName.get(r.t.n); const R = RV.get(r); return `<button type="button" class="chtile l${r.level}${R ? ' rv-' + R.c : ''}" ${attr}="${esc(r.t.n)}" aria-label="${esc(r.t.d)}: ${esc(rvWord(r))}" title="${esc(r.t.d)}: ${esc(rvWord(r))}">${sprite(p) || `<span class="chk">${esc(r.t.d.slice(0, 3))}</span>`}</button>`; }).join('')}</div>` : ''}`; };
// suggestions on the report: the top fix plans, one tap to apply (same plans as Build › Swaps)
// the first suggestion shows; Show more opens the next two in place, then See all goes to the Suggestions page (Nov 7, Sim)
CH.sugs = () => { let L = ui.planList || [], side = false; const base = ui.vsum && ui.vsum.RD ? ui.vsum.RD.score : null;
  // the grade (the big number) each suggestion would give, so the card matches what Apply does (Nov 7, Sim)
  let g0 = ui.plans && ui.plans.gBase != null ? ui.plans.gBase : null; if (g0 == null) { try { const ms = members(); g0 = ms.length === 6 ? gradeTeam(ms) : null; } catch (e) {} }
  // nothing makes the team readier: show the side-grades (different picks that keep every check) instead of a dead end
  // (Nov 7 audit) only ones about as ready as now (at most 2 points less) and never a Pokémon you just swapped out with Apply,
  // so following them can't go round in circles or drift downhill
  if (ui.plans && !ui.plans.length && Array.isArray(ui.plans.more) && ui.plans.more.length) { side = true;
    const ok = CH.sideGrades(ui.plans.more, g0, base);
    L = ui.planList = ok;
    if (!ok.length) return `<section class="chsugs" aria-label="Suggestions"><p class="t2 chsidek">Nothing raises this team’s Strength Score right now, and every other swap would lower it.</p><button type="button" class="linkbtn chseeall" data-view="coverage">See all suggestions ›</button></section>`; }
  const top = L.slice(0, 3); let tk = ''; try { tk = members().map(m => m.p.n).join(','); } catch (e) {} const open = ui.sugMore === tk, hid = top.length - 1; const gapN = ui.plans && ui.plans.gapNote ? `<p class="chgapnote">${esc(ui.plans.gapNote)}</p>` : ''; // a game-plan gap nothing in this pool can fix
  /* Part 3 (Nov 10): a Protect fix (a set change, not a swap) and "Nothing to fix" sit above the cards (36-lanes.js) */
  let pre = ''; try { const ms = members(); pre = LANE.protCard(ms) + (ui.plans && ui.plans.nothing ? LANE.noneCard() : ''); } catch (e) {}
  if (!top.length) return gapN || pre ? `<section class="chsugs" aria-label="Suggestions">${pre}${gapN}</section>` : ''; // top 2 here; All suggestions has the rest
  return `<section class="chsugs${open ? ' is-more' : ''}" aria-label="Suggestions">${pre}${gapN}${side ? '<p class="t2 chsidek">Nothing raises this team’s Strength Score right now. These swaps keep every check and about the same grade, with a different look:</p>' : ''}
    ${top.map((pl, k) => { if (pl._ts === undefined && pl.team && pl.team.length === 6) { try { pl._ts = tsOf(pl.team); } catch (e) { pl._ts = null; } } const d = pl._ts != null && base != null ? pl._ts - base : null;
      const pics = pl.ch.map(x => `${x.out ? `<span class="o">${sprite(x.out.p) || ''}</span><span class="ar" aria-hidden="true">→</span>` : '<span class="ar" aria-hidden="true">+</span>'}<span class="i">${sprite(x.c.p) || ''}</span>`).join('');
      const words = pl.ch.map(x => `<b>${esc(x.c.p.d)}</b>${x.out ? ` <span class="for">for ${esc(x.out.d)}</span>` : ' <span class="for">added</span>'}`).join('<br>');
      // Why?: the same reasons the full plan card gives, one tap away
      const why = (pl._why && pl._why.length ? pl._why : side ? ['Keeps every check and your speed control', 'About the same grade as now, with a different look'] : []);
      const wk = pl._wk ? `<li>Weak spots: <b>${esc(pl._wk[0])}</b> → <b>${esc(pl._wk[1])}</b></li>` : '';
      const nr = why.length + (wk ? 1 : 0);
      const whyH = nr ? `<details class="chwhy"><summary><span class="q">Why?</span><span class="n">${nr} reason${nr === 1 ? '' : 's'}</span></summary><ul>${wk}${why.map(l => `<li>${l}</li>`).join('')}</ul></details>` : '';
      const g1 = g0 != null ? planGrade(pl) : null, dlH = g1 != null ? gradeChip(g0, g1, 'dl', pl.team) : d != null ? `<span class="dl ${d > 0 ? 'up' : d < 0 ? 'dn' : ''}">${readyDelta(d)}</span>` : '', apH = `<button type="button" class="${k ? 'chghost' : 'chprimary'} chapply" data-plan="${k}">Apply</button>`;
      // two or more changes: one row per change, then the readiness and Apply underneath (Oct 30, Sim: the squeezed column looked unpolished)
      if (pl.ch.length > 1) return `<div class="chsugw"><div class="chsug multi"><div class="chml">${pl.ch.map(x => `<div class="chmc"><div class="pics">${x.out ? `<span class="o">${sprite(x.out.p) || ''}</span><span class="ar" aria-hidden="true">→</span>` : '<span class="ar" aria-hidden="true">+</span>'}<span class="i">${sprite(x.c.p) || ''}</span></div><div class="txt"><b>${esc(x.c.p.d)}</b>${x.out ? ` <span class="for">for ${esc(x.out.d)}</span>` : ' <span class="for">added</span>'}</div></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="chmf">${dlH}${apH}</div>${LANE.line(pl)}</div>${whyH}</div>`;
      return `<div class="chsugw"><div class="chsug"><div class="pics">${pics}</div><div class="txt">${words}${dlH}${LANE.line(pl)}</div>${apH}</div>${whyH}</div>`; }).join('')}${hid > 0 && !open ? `<button type="button" class="linkbtn chseeall" data-sugmore aria-expanded="false">Show ${hid} more suggestion${hid === 1 ? '' : 's'}</button>` : `<button type="button" class="linkbtn chseeall" data-view="coverage">See all suggestions ›</button>`}</section>`; };
// Show more: the next suggestions open in place (the same list, already worked out); the button becomes See all suggestions
document.addEventListener('click', ev => { const b = ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('[data-sugmore]'); if (!b) return; const sec = b.closest('.chsugs'); if (!sec) return;
  try { ui.sugMore = members().map(m => m.p.n).join(','); } catch (e) {} sec.classList.add('is-more');
  const a = document.createElement('button'); a.type = 'button'; a.className = b.className; a.dataset.view = 'coverage'; a.textContent = 'See all suggestions ›'; b.replaceWith(a);
  const nx = sec.querySelectorAll('.chsugw')[1], f = nx && nx.querySelector('.chapply'); if (f) f.focus({preventScroll: true}); });
CH.legendRow = RD => { const c = CH.lvCount(RD); let rv = null; try { rv = RD.rows.map(r => CH.realVs(r.t.n)).filter(Boolean); } catch (e) {}
  if (rv && rv.length) { const k = w => rv.filter(x => x.c === w).length; return `<div class="chleg chlegrv"><span><i class="rv-bad" aria-hidden="true"></i>Unfavoured ${k('bad')}</span><span><i class="rv-mid" aria-hidden="true"></i>Even match ${k('mid')}</span><span><i class="rv-ok" aria-hidden="true"></i>Favoured ${k('ok')}</span></div><p class="t2 chlegcalc">Damage calc: ${CH.LV[0]} ${c[0]} · ${CH.LV[1]} ${c[1]} · ${CH.LV[2]} ${c[2]} · ${CH.LV[3]} ${c[3]}</p>`; }
  return `<div class="chleg">${[[0, 'var(--chbad)', '✗', 'bad'], [1, 'var(--chshaky)', '~', 'part'], [2, 'var(--chmid)', '✓', 'mid'], [3, 'var(--chok)', '✓✓', 'ok']].map(([l, col, g, k]) => `<span><i class="g-${k}" style="background:${col}" aria-hidden="true">${g}</i>${CH.LV[l]} ${c[l]}</span>`).join('')}</div>`; };

// ---------- Home ----------
const chHomeOld = renderHome;
renderHome = function () { const el = $('homeOut'); if (!el) return; const pane = document.querySelector('[data-pane="home"]'); if (pane && pane.hidden && el.innerHTML) return;
  el.classList.add('chhome');
  const ms = members(), v = ui.vsum || {}, RD = ms.length ? prepOf(ms) : null, six = CH.metaSix(), F = CH.four(ms), nTeams = (RES && RES.teams) || 0;
  const byN = n => ms.find(m => m.p.n === n), fl = F ? F.lead.map(byN).filter(Boolean) : [], fb = F ? F.back.map(byN).filter(Boolean) : [];
  const tagOf = m => !F ? '' : fl.includes(m) ? '<div class="chtag lead">Lead</div>' : fb.includes(m) ? '<div class="chtag">Back</div>' : '<div class="chtag">Bench</div>';
  const mine = ms.slice(0, 6), ord = F ? [...fl, ...fb, ...mine.filter(m => !fl.includes(m) && !fb.includes(m))] : mine;
  const you = `<div class="chband you chhalf"><div class="chk">● You${ms.length ? ' · ' + esc(teamTitleFor(ms)) : ''}</div><div class="chsix">${ms.length ? ord.map(m => `<div role="button" tabindex="0" data-lt="${esc(m.p.n)}" aria-label="${esc(m.d)}: open its info">${sprite(m.p) || ''}${tagOf(m)}</div>`).join('') : Array.from({length: 6}, () => '<div><div class="empty"></div></div>').join('')}</div>${ms.length ? '' : '<div class="chk" style="margin-top:6px">Your six goes here</div>'}</div>`;

CH.seg = (key, opts, on) => `<div class="seg chseg" role="group" data-chseg="${key}">${opts.map(([k, t]) => `<button type="button" data-chsegv="${k}" aria-pressed="${k === on}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
CH.part = (id, n, title, q) => { const s = document.createElement('section'); s.className = 'chpart'; s.id = id; s.innerHTML = `<div class="chparth"><span class="n">${n}</span><div><h2 class="chdisp">${title}</h2><p>${q}</p></div></div><div class="chpartb"></div>`; return s; };
document.addEventListener('click', e => { const b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-chsegv]'); if (!b) return; const g = b.closest('[data-chseg]'), key = g.dataset.chseg, v = b.dataset.chsegv;
  LS.set('cc2.seg.' + key, v); g.querySelectorAll('[data-chsegv]').forEach(x => x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', x === b)); document.querySelectorAll(`[data-chsegp="${key}"]`).forEach(p => { p.hidden = p.dataset.chsegk !== v; }); safe('page parts', lazyFlush); });
function chTeamInit() { const rp = document.querySelector('[data-pane="report"]'); if (!rp || rp.dataset.ch) return; rp.dataset.ch = '1';
  const team = $('team'), fixes = $('fixes'), rep = $('repFold'); if (!team || !fixes || !rep) return;
  const cols = fixes.querySelectorAll('.fixcol');
  const mk = (key, title, open) => { const w = document.createElement('div'); w.innerHTML = CH.fold(key, title, '', '', open); const d = w.firstElementChild; d.querySelector('.chs') || d.querySelector('summary > div').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="chs"></div>'); return d; };
  const flat = (sid, keepFold) => { const s = $(sid); if (!s) return null; const h = s.querySelector(':scope > .head'); if (h) { const p = h.querySelector('.hint'); if (p) { p.classList.add('t2'); h.before(p); } h.remove(); } if (!keepFold) s.querySelectorAll('.phonefold').forEach(x => { x.open = true; x.classList.add('chflat'); }); return s; };
  const panel = (key, k, nodes, on) => { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.dataset.chsegp = key; d.dataset.chsegk = k; d.hidden = k !== on; nodes.filter(Boolean).forEach(n => d.append(n)); return d; };
  const head = document.createElement('div'); head.id = 'chTeam'; head.className = 'chteam'; rp.prepend(head);
  // 1 · Fix it: suggestions, then everything still to look at (problems, checklist, legal and items)
  const p1 = CH.part('chP1', '1', 'Suggestions', 'What to change, best first'); const b1 = p1.querySelector('.chpartb');
  b1.innerHTML = '<div class="chpoolrow"><span class="chk">Suggest from</span><div class="seg chpool" role="group" aria-label="Suggest from"><button type="button" data-chpool="box">My box</button><button type="button" data-chpool="boxfill">Box + catch</button><button type="button" data-chpool="all">Whole roster</button></div></div>'+/* the Mega choices, here as well as in Plan settings (Sim, Nov 7: they had only stayed on All suggestions and Build) */'<div class="chmegarow"><div class="chpoolrow"><span class="chk">Megas on team</span><div class="seg chpool" role="group" aria-label="Megas on the team"><button type="button" data-megas="0">0</button><button type="button" data-megas="1">1</button><button type="button" data-megas="2">2</button></div></div><div class="chpoolrow"><span class="chk">Swap my Mega?</span><div class="seg chpool" role="group" aria-label="Suggestions can swap out my Mega"><button type="button" data-chswapm="0">No</button><button type="button" data-chswapm="1">Yes</button></div></div></div><div id="chSugOut"></div>'; if (cols[0]) { const h = cols[0].querySelector(':scope > .head h2'); if (h) h.remove(); }
  const fx = mk('rfix', 'Problems and team checks', false); fx.id = 'chfFix'; const fb = fx.querySelector('.chbody'); [cols[0], $('chkCard'), $('verdict')].filter(Boolean).forEach(n => fb.append(n)); b1.append(fx);
  // 2 · Threats: one section, three views of the same 30 threats

    [...foot.children].filter(x => x !== s).forEach(x => body.append(x)); foot.append(body); if (s) s.innerHTML = `${CH.tog()}<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="cht">How scoring works</div></div>`; }

  const ed = document.createElement('div'); ed.className = 'chedit'; ed.id = 'chEdit'; ed.hidden = true; ed.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); ed.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); ed.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Edit team');
