  document.body.append(scrim, sh); scrim.addEventListener('click', () => chScoutClose()); }
// open the editor on one slot (or the whole team for import, export and saving)
function chEditOpen(i) { const ed = $('chEdit'); if (!ed) return; ed.dataset.slot = i == null ? 'all' : String(i);
  if (ui.tview === 'grid') { ui.tview = 'full'; LS.set('cc2.tview', 'full'); try { renderSlots(); } catch (e) {} }
  const m = i == null ? null : members().find(x => x.i === +i); $('chEditT').textContent = i == null ? 'Edit team' : m ? 'Edit ' + m.d : 'Add a Pokémon';
  ed.classList.toggle('chadd', i != null && !m); ed.hidden = false; $('chEditScrim').hidden = false; document.documentElement.classList.add('chlock'); ed.scrollTop = 0; { const eb = ed.querySelector('.chedb'); if (eb) eb.scrollTop = 0; }
  // its set editor, drawn now if an edit since it was last drawn changed the team
  safe('set editor', slotsFlush);
  // an empty slot: focus its name box right away, inside the tap, so phones open the keyboard
  try { chEditStrip(); } catch (e) {}
  if (i != null && !m) { const inp = ed.querySelector(`#pk${i}`) || ed.querySelector(`#slot${i} input`); if (inp) { try { inp.focus({preventScroll: true}); } catch (e) { inp.focus(); } } }
  else { try { ed.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1'); ed.focus({preventScroll: true}); } catch (e) {} } } /* focus moves into the editor, not left on the card behind */
// the edit panel's team strip: your six as mini sprites (tap to switch), + for an empty slot; and a clear Remove button
function chEditStrip() { const ed = $('chEdit'); if (!ed || ed.hidden) return; let st = ed.querySelector('.chedstrip'); if (!st) { st = document.createElement('div'); st.className = 'chedstrip'; st.setAttribute('role', 'group'); st.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Your six'); ed.querySelector('.chedh').after(st); }
  const cur = ed.dataset.slot, mm = new Map(members().map(m => [m.i, m]));
  st.innerHTML = S.slots.map((x, i) => { const m = mm.get(i); return `<button type="button" class="chst${String(i) === cur ? ' on' : ''}${m ? '' : ' add'}" data-chedit="${i}" aria-label="${m ? 'Edit ' + esc(m.d) : 'Add a Pokémon in slot ' + (i + 1)}"${String(i) === cur ? ' aria-current="true"' : ''}>${m ? (sprite(m.p, 'mini') || esc(m.d.slice(0, 3))) : '<span aria-hidden="true">+</span>'}</button>`; }).join('');
  let rb = ed.querySelector('.chedrm'); const m = cur !== 'all' ? mm.get(+cur) : null;
  if (!rb) { rb = document.createElement('button'); rb.type = 'button'; rb.className = 'chghost chedrm'; ed.querySelector('.chedb').append(rb); }
  rb.hidden = !m; if (m) { rb.dataset.chrm = cur; rb.textContent = `Remove ${m.d} from the team`; }
  // the title follows the slot: after adding a Pokémon it says Edit <name>, not Add a Pokémon
  { const t = $('chEditT'); if (t) t.textContent = cur === 'all' ? 'Edit team' : m ? 'Edit ' + m.d : 'Add a Pokémon'; ed.classList.toggle('chadd', cur !== 'all' && !m); } }
function chEditClose() { const ed = $('chEdit'); if (!ed || ed.hidden) return; ui.saving = false; ui.paste = null; /* the next Save/Import opens its panel again (sweep, Oct 31) */ ed.hidden = true; $('chEditScrim').hidden = true; document.documentElement.classList.remove('chlock'); }

function chTeamRender() { const head = $('chTeam'); if (!head) return;
  /* Nov 10: the all-sets fold sits in the team section between draws; return it to its part before anything is redrawn */
  { const keep = $('chSets'), p4b = document.querySelector('#chP4 .chpartb'); if (keep && p4b && !p4b.contains(keep)) p4b.append(keep); }
  const ms = members(), RD = ms.length ? prepOf(ms) : null, v = ui.vsum || {}, c = CH.lvCount(RD); const G = RD ? Object.assign(CH.gradeOf(RD, v.CK, ms), {r: RD.score}) : null, hd = CH.hand(RD);
  const F = CH.four(ms), lead = F ? ms.find(m => m.p.n === F.lead[0]) || null : null, ko = CH.koNote(RD);
  const note = m => { if (lead && m === lead) { const w = CH.leadWhy(m); return `<div class="chpen">↑ Lead vs the meta's six${w ? ' · ' + esc(w) : ''}</div>`; } if (ko && m.p.n === ko.n && !(lead && lead.p.n === ko.n)) return `<div class="chpen">${esc(ko.txt)}</div>`; return ''; };
  const all = S.slots.map((s, i) => ({m: ms.find(x => x.i === i), i})).slice(0, 6);
  // a filled card is a tappable box (not a button) so it can hold its own − (remove) button
  const row = ({m, i}) => m ? `<div role="button" tabindex="0" class="chrow tyx" style="${tyVars(m.p)}" data-chedit="${i}" aria-label="Edit ${esc(m.d)}"><button type="button" class="chrm" data-chrm="${i}" aria-label="Remove ${esc(m.d)} from the team">−</button><span class="ix">${i + 1}</span>${sprite(m.p) || ''}<div class="chbd"><div class="nm">${esc(m.d)}${m.spread && m.spread.assumed ? ' <span class="chtag ass" title="No stat points on the sheet: your nature on the most common spread">spread assumed</span>' : ''}</div><div class="mono">${esc(m.item || 'No item')} · ${esc(m.ability || '')}${memberSpread(m).nat ? ' · ' + esc(memberSpread(m).nat) + ' nature' : ''}</div><div class="mono mv">${m.moves.map(x => esc(x.name)).join(' · ')}</div>${note(m)}</div><span class="chedi" aria-hidden="true">Edit</span></div>`
    : `<button type="button" class="chrow empty" data-chedit="${i}" aria-label="Add a Pokémon to slot ${i + 1}"><span class="ix">${i + 1}</span><span class="chadd">+</span><div class="chbd"><div class="nm">Add a Pokémon</div></div></button>`;
  const ck = v.CK ? `<span class="chpill ${v.CK.pass === v.CK.n ? 'ok' : ''}">${v.CK.pass} of ${v.CK.n} team checks</span>` : '';
  const legal = ms.length ? `<span class="chpill ${v.legal === false ? 'bad' : 'ok'}" title="Based on our reading of the ${esc(regName())} rules. Check the official rules before an event.">${v.legal === false ? 'Not legal yet' : 'Legal'}</span>` : '';
  head.innerHTML = `<div class="chthead"><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="chk">My team · ${ms.length} of 6</div><div class="nm" role="heading" aria-level="1">${esc(ms.length ? teamTitleFor(ms) : 'No team yet')}</div>
      ${G ? `<div class="rzgrade" data-band="${G.band || String(G.letter)[0]}"><span class="rzgn">${G.v === 2 ? Math.round(G.s) : esc(G.letter)}</span><span class="rzgl">${G.v === 2 ? esc(CH.wordOfL(G.letter)) + (G.partial ? ' so far' : '') : CH.gradeNum(G)}</span><button type="button" class="rzwhy" data-chgrade="1" aria-expanded="${ui.gwhy ? 'true' : 'false'}" aria-controls="chGwhy">${G.v === 2 ? 'Why this score?' : `Why ${G.letter}?`}</button></div>` : ''}
      ${RD ? CH.recStats(G, RD, hd, c) : `<div class="rd">Add your six and NastyPlot scouts the top 30 threats for you.</div>`}
      <div class="chpills">${legal}${ck}</div>
      ${ms.length ? `<div class="chsix6 rzsix" role="group" aria-label="Your six: tap one to see its set">${all.filter(x => x.m).map(({m, i}) => `<button type="button" data-chset="${i}" aria-pressed="${ui.rzSet === i ? 'true' : 'false'}" aria-controls="rzSet" aria-label="${esc(m.d)}: show its set">${sprite(m.p) || ''}<span>${esc(m.d)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
      <div class="chacts2" id="chActs"><button type="button" class="chghost" data-view="teams">Saved teams</button><button type="button" class="chghost" data-view="box">My box</button>${ms.length ? '<button type="button" class="chghost" data-cxgo="cur|">Compare with…</button>' : ''}${ms.length && typeof sdBtn === 'function' ? sdBtn('mine') : ''}</div></div>
    </div>
    ${ms.length ? `<div id="rzSet" class="rzset${ui.rzSet != null && all[ui.rzSet] && all[ui.rzSet].m ? ` tyx" style="${tyVars(all[ui.rzSet].m.p)}` : ''}" aria-live="polite"${ui.rzSet != null && all[ui.rzSet] && all[ui.rzSet].m ? '' : ' hidden'}>${typeof rzSetHtml === 'function' && ui.rzSet != null && all[ui.rzSet] && all[ui.rzSet].m ? rzSetHtml(all[ui.rzSet].m, ui.rzSet) : ''}</div>` : ''}
    ${G ? CH.gradeWhy(G, {short: 1}).replace('id="chGwhy"', `id="chGwhy"${ui.gwhy ? '' : ' hidden'}`) : ''}
    ${ms.length ? '' : `<div class="chempty2"><button type="button" class="chprimary" data-chedit="0">Add your first Pokémon <span aria-hidden="true">➜</span></button><button type="button" class="chghost" data-view="auto">Build me a team</button></div>`}`;
  if (CH.acts) $('chActs').append(CH.acts);
  safe('grade motion', () => CH.gradeMotion(head, G, RD));
  document.querySelectorAll('#chP1,#chP2,#chP3').forEach(p => { p.hidden = !ms.length; });
  // empty team: nothing to save, share, export or clear (Import stays in ⋯), and "Your six" is the only part, so no step number
  ['saveBtn', 'shareBtn', 'exportBtn', 'clearTeam'].forEach(id => { const x = $(id); if (x) x.hidden = !ms.length; });
  { const n = document.querySelector('#chP4 .chparth .n'); if (n) n.hidden = !ms.length; const q = document.querySelector('#chP4 .chparth p'); if (q) q.textContent = ms.length ? 'Tap a Pokémon to edit its set' : 'Tap a slot to add a Pokémon'; }
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-chpool]').forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.chpool === ui.pool));
  { const sw = $('swapMega'); document.querySelectorAll('[data-chswapm]').forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!!(sw && sw.checked) === (b.dataset.chswapm === '1')))); }
  // part 1: suggestions
  { const o = $('chSugOut'); if (o && !o.previousElementSibling?.classList.contains('chundohost')) { const u = document.createElement('div'); u.className = 'chundohost'; o.before(u); } undoBarSync(); }
  { const o = $('chSugOut'); if (o && ms.length && ui.plans && ui.plans.pending) planStale(o, PLAN_WAIT.replace('the best fixes', 'suggestions')); else if (o) { planFresh(o); o.innerHTML = ms.length ? (CH.sugs() || '<p class="t2">No single change from this pool raises the team’s Strength Score. Try another pool above, or see Threats below for what to watch.</p>') : ''; } }
  const sum = (id, t) => { const d = $(id); const s = d && d.querySelector('.chs'); if (s) s.textContent = t; };
  { const nd = $('needs'), n = $('needsSub'); sum('chfFix', [n && n.textContent ? n.textContent : 'Nothing big to fix', v.CK ? `${v.CK.pass}/${v.CK.n} checks` : '', v.legal === false ? 'not legal yet' : 'legal'].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')); }
  { const g = document.querySelector('#chfGplan .gpcard'), tags = g ? [...g.querySelectorAll('.gphead .tag, .gphead .chip, .gphead .arch')].map(x => x.textContent.trim()).filter(Boolean) : []; sum('chfGplan', (tags.length ? tags.map(x => x.charAt(0) + x.slice(1).toLowerCase()).join(' + ') + ' · ' : '') + 'leads, backups and what to watch'); }
  // part 2: threats
  { const o = $('chThrTop'); if (o) o.innerHTML = RD ? `<div class="chside">${CH.threats10(RD, 'data-chthr')}<div class="chlegk">${CH.legendRow(RD)}</div></div>` : ''; const g = $('chVsOut'); if (g) g.innerHTML = RD ? CH.chart(RD, ms) : ''; }
  // part 4: the sheet, every slot (empty ones say Add)

      : `<div class="chsheet"><div class="hd"><span class="chdisp">Open team sheet</span><span class="mono">Reg M-C, bring 6, pick 4</span></div>${all.map(row).join('')}</div>`; }
  if (!$('chScout').hidden) chScoutRender(); try { chEditStrip(); } catch (e) {} }

// the top plan, as one line (the plans themselves are worked out by the fix planner)
function chBestPlan() { const L = ui.planList || []; const pl = L[0]; if (!pl || !pl.ch) return null; const base = ui.vsum && ui.vsum.RD ? ui.vsum.RD.score : null;
  return {pl, txt: pl.ch.map(x => x.out ? `${x.out.d} → ${x.c.p.d}` : `add ${x.c.p.d}`).join(', '), d: pl._ts != null && base != null ? pl._ts - base : null, g0: ui.plans && ui.plans.gBase, g1: planGrade(pl)}; }
// ---------- the scouting report bottom sheet ----------
CH.sel = null; CH.last = null;
function chScoutOpen(sel) { const sh = $('chScout'); if (!sh) return; CH.sel = sel || null; CH.last = document.activeElement; chScoutRender(); sh.hidden = false; $('chScrim').hidden = false;
  const x = sh.querySelector('.x'); if (x) x.focus(); }
function chScoutClose() { const sh = $('chScout'); if (!sh || sh.hidden) return; sh.hidden = true; $('chScrim').hidden = true; try { if (CH.last && CH.last.focus) CH.last.focus(); } catch (e) {} }
function chScoutRender() { const sh = $('chScout'); if (!sh) return; const ms = members(), RD = ms.length ? prepOf(ms) : null, v = ui.vsum || {};
  if (!RD) { sh.innerHTML = '<div class="grab"></div><button type="button" class="x" data-chclose aria-label="Close">×</button><div class="chh" role="heading" aria-level="2">Scouting report</div><p class="t2">Add Pokémon to your team to see how ready it is for the top threats.</p>'; return; }
  const need = CH.needN(RD), G = CH.gradeOf(RD, v.CK, ms), sel = CH.sel && RD.rows.find(r => r.t.n === CH.sel);
  const dot = (r, m) => { const k = CH.LVC[CH.cellOf(r, m)];
    return `<span title="${esc(m.d)}: ${k[1]}">${sprite(m.p) || ''}<i class="g-${k[3]}" style="background:${k[0]}" aria-hidden="true">${k[2]}</i>${k[1]}</span>`; };
  const det = sel ? `<div class="chdet" aria-live="polite"><div class="top">${sprite(byName.get(sel.t.n)) || ''}<div style="flex:1"><div class="nm" style="font:800 15px var(--body);text-transform:uppercase">${esc(sel.t.d)} <span class="chk">${pctTxt(+sel.t.w)} of top cuts</span></div><div class="t2">${esc(THR.evidence(sel))}</div></div><span class="chlv l${sel.level}">${CH.LV[sel.level].toUpperCase()}</span></div><div class="chdots">${ms.map(m => dot(sel, m)).join('')}</div></div>` : '';
  const list = CH.worst5(RD).map(r => `<div class="it">${sprite(byName.get(r.t.n)) || ''}<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="nm">${esc(r.t.d)} <span class="chk" style="font-weight:400">${pctTxt(+r.t.w)} of top cuts</span></div><div class="t2">${esc(THR.evidence(r))}</div></div><span class="chlv l${r.level}">${CH.LV[r.level].toUpperCase()}</span></div>`).join('');
  const ck = v.CK ? `<div class="chk" style="margin-top:14px">Team checks · ${v.CK.pass}/${v.CK.n}</div><div class="chleg">${v.CK.items.filter(x => !x.skip && !x.note).map(x => `<span><i class="g-${x.ok ? 'ok' : 'bad'}" style="background:${x.ok ? 'var(--chok)' : 'var(--chbad)'}" role="img" aria-label="${x.ok ? 'yes' : 'no'}">${x.ok ? '✓' : '✗'}</i>${esc(x.label)}</span>`).join('')}</div>` : '';
  const b = chBestPlan();
  const best = b ? `<div class="chbest"><div class="chk">Best fix${b.g0 != null && b.g1 != null ? ` · ${CH.gradeMove(b.g0, b.g1)}` : b.d != null ? ` · ${readyDelta(b.d)}` : ''}</div><div class="sw">${b.pl.ch.map(x => `${x.out ? `${sprite(x.out.p, 'out') || ''}<span aria-hidden="true" style="color:var(--chyel);font-size:22px">→</span>` : '+'}${sprite(x.c.p) || ''}`).join(' ')}<span>${esc(b.txt)}</span></div><button type="button" class="chprimary" data-plan="0">Apply this fix <span aria-hidden="true">➜</span></button></div>` : '';
  sh.innerHTML = `<div class="grab"></div><button type="button" class="x" data-chclose aria-label="Close">×</button>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding-right:48px"><div style="flex:1"><div class="chk">Scouting report · top 30 threats</div><div class="chh" role="heading" aria-level="2">${need ? `${need} threat${need === 1 ? '' : 's'} need${need === 1 ? 's' : ''} <em>a plan.</em>` : 'Ready for <em>every</em> top threat.'}</div></div><div class="chring sm gc" style="--gc:${CH.gcol(G.letter)}" data-band="${G.band || String(G.letter)[0]}"><span class="chrl">${G.letter}</span><small>${G.rec ? Math.round(G.rec.wr * 100) + '% wins' : G.s}</small></div></div>
    <div style="font-size:15px;color:var(--chink2);margin-top:6px"><span style="color:var(--chink)">${G.partial ? `<b>${CH.readyTxt(RD)}</b> so far` : CH.gradeSum(G, RD)}</span> · ${CH.handTxt(RD)} · ${CH.LEGEND}</div>
    ${CH.legendRow(RD)}${CH.tiles(RD, 'data-chthr')}${det}
    ${list ? `<div class="chlist" style="margin-top:8px">${list}</div>` : ''}${ck}${best}
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;margin-top:12px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button type="button" class="chghost" data-chfull="">Full report</button><button type="button" class="chghost" data-chclose="">Close</button></div>`;
  sh.querySelectorAll('[data-chthr]').forEach(t => t.setAttribute('aria-pressed', t.dataset.chthr === CH.sel ? 'true' : 'false')); }
document.addEventListener('click', e => { const t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
